   525→// GetRemoteMetadataBranchTree returns the tree object for origin/entire/checkpoints/v1.
   526→func GetRemoteMetadataBranchTree(repo *git.Repository) (*object.Tree, error) {
   527→	refName := plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", paths.MetadataBranchName)
   528→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   529→	if err != nil {
   530→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get remote metadata branch reference: %w", err)
   531→	}
   532→
   533→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   534→	if err != nil {
   535→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get remote metadata branch commit: %w", err)
   536→	}
   537→
   538→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   539→	if err != nil {
   540→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get remote metadata branch tree: %w", err)
   541→	}
   542→	return tree, nil
   543→}
   544→
   545→// OpenRepository opens the git repository with linked worktree support enabled.
   546→// It uses git.PlainOpenWithOptions with EnableDotGitCommonDir set to true,
   547→// which is required for proper operation in git worktrees created via 'git worktree add'.
   548→//
   549→// Without EnableDotGitCommonDir, go-git operations in worktrees can silently fail:
   550→// - Commits appear to succeed but are not persisted
   551→// - Refs are written to incorrect locations
   552→// - The worktree's HEAD/index don't get updated properly
   553→//
   554→// This happens because worktrees use .git as a file (pointing to the main repo)
   555→// rather than a directory, and go-git needs to route paths correctly between
   556→// shared (.git/) and per-worktree (.git/worktrees/<name>/) locations.
   557→//
   558→// The function first uses 'git rev-parse --show-toplevel' to find the repository
   559→// root, which works correctly even when called from a subdirectory within the repo.
   560→func OpenRepository() (*git.Repository, error) {
   561→	// First, find the repository root using git rev-parse --show-toplevel
   562→	// This works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository
   563→	repoRoot, err := GetWorktreePath()
   564→	if err != nil {
   565→		// Fallback to current directory if git command fails
   566→		// (e.g., if git is not installed or we're not in a repo)
   567→		repoRoot = "."
   568→	}
   569→
   570→	repo, err := git.PlainOpenWithOptions(repoRoot, &git.PlainOpenOptions{
   571→		EnableDotGitCommonDir: true,
   572→	})
   573→	if err != nil {
   574→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   575→	}
   576→	return repo, nil
   577→}
   578→
   579→// IsInsideWorktree returns true if the current directory is inside a git worktree
   580→// (as opposed to the main repository). Worktrees have .git as a file pointing
   581→// to the main repo, while the main repo has .git as a directory.
   582→// This function works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
   583→func IsInsideWorktree() bool {
   584→	// First find the repository root
   585→	repoRoot, err := GetWorktreePath()
   586→	if err != nil {
   587→		return false
   588→	}
   589→
   590→	gitPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, gitDir)
   591→	gitInfo, err := os.Stat(gitPath)
   592→	if err != nil {
   593→		return false
   594→	}
   595→	return !gitInfo.IsDir()
   596→}
   597→
   598→// GetMainRepoRoot returns the root directory of the main repository.
   599→// In the main repo, this is the worktree path (repo root).
   600→// In a worktree, this parses the .git file to find the main repo.
   601→// This function works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
   602→//
   603→// Per gitrepository-layout(5), a worktree's .git file is a "gitfile" containing
   604→// "gitdir: <path>" pointing to $GIT_DIR/worktrees/<id> in the main repository.
   605→// See: https://git-scm.com/docs/gitrepository-layout
   606→func GetMainRepoRoot() (string, error) {
   607→	// First find the worktree/repo root
   608→	repoRoot, err := GetWorktreePath()
   609→	if err != nil {
   610→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree path: %w", err)
   611→	}
   612→
   613→	if !IsInsideWorktree() {
   614→		return repoRoot, nil
   615→	}
   616→
   617→	// Worktree .git file contains: "gitdir: /path/to/main/.git/worktrees/<id>"
   618→	gitFilePath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, gitDir)
   619→	content, err := os.ReadFile(gitFilePath) //nolint:gosec // G304: gitFilePath is constructed from repo root, not user input
   620→	if err != nil {
   621→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read .git file: %w", err)
   622→	}
   623→
   624→	gitdir := strings.TrimSpace(string(content))
   625→	gitdir = strings.TrimPrefix(gitdir, "gitdir: ")
   626→
   627→	// Extract main repo root: everything before "/.git/"
   628→	idx := strings.LastIndex(gitdir, "/.git/")
   629→	if idx < 0 {
   630→		return "", fmt.Errorf("unexpected gitdir format: %s", gitdir)
   631→	}
   632→	return gitdir[:idx], nil
   633→}
   634→
   635→// GetGitCommonDir returns the path to the shared git directory.
   636→// In a regular checkout, this is .git/
   637→// In a worktree, this is the main repo's .git/ (not .git/worktrees/<name>/)
   638→// Uses git rev-parse --git-common-dir for reliable handling of worktrees.
   639→func GetGitCommonDir() (string, error) {
   640→	ctx := context.Background()
   641→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--git-common-dir")
   642→	cmd.Dir = "."
   643→	output, err := cmd.Output()
   644→	if err != nil {
   645→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get git common dir: %w", err)
   646→	}
   647→
   648→	commonDir := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
   649→
   650→	// git rev-parse --git-common-dir returns relative paths from the working directory,
   651→	// so we need to make it absolute if it isn't already
   652→	if !filepath.IsAbs(commonDir) {
   653→		commonDir = filepath.Join(".", commonDir)
   654→	}
   655→
   656→	return filepath.Clean(commonDir), nil
   657→}
   658→
   659→// GetWorktreePath returns the absolute path to the current worktree root.
   660→// This is the working directory path, not the git directory.
   661→func GetWorktreePath() (string, error) {
   662→	ctx := context.Background()
   663→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel")
   664→	output, err := cmd.Output()
   665→	if err != nil {
   666→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree path: %w", err)
   667→	}
   668→	return strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), nil
   669→}
   670→
   671→// EnsureEntireGitignore ensures all required entries are in .entire/.gitignore
   672→// Works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
   673→func EnsureEntireGitignore() error {
   674→	// Get absolute path for the gitignore file
   675→	gitignoreAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(entireGitignore)
   676→	if err != nil {
   677→		gitignoreAbs = entireGitignore // Fallback to relative
   678→	}
   679→
   680→	// Read existing content
   681→	var content string
   682→	if data, err := os.ReadFile(gitignoreAbs); err == nil { //nolint:gosec // path is from AbsPath or constant
   683→		content = string(data)
   684→	}
   685→
   686→	// All entries that should be in .entire/.gitignore
   687→	requiredEntries := []string{
   688→		"tmp/",
   689→		"settings.local.json",
   690→		"metadata/",
   691→		"logs/",
   692→	}
   693→
   694→	// Track what needs to be added
   695→	var toAdd []string
   696→	for _, entry := range requiredEntries {
   697→		if !strings.Contains(content, entry) {
   698→			toAdd = append(toAdd, entry)
   699→		}
   700→	}
   701→
   702→	// Nothing to add
   703→	if len(toAdd) == 0 {
   704→		return nil
   705→	}
   706→
   707→	// Ensure .entire directory exists
   708→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(gitignoreAbs), 0o750); err != nil {
   709→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create .entire directory: %w", err)
   710→	}
   711→
   712→	// Append missing entries to gitignore
   713→	var sb strings.Builder
   714→	for _, entry := range toAdd {
   715→		sb.WriteString(entry + "\n")
   716→	}
   717→	content += sb.String()
   718→
   719→	if err := os.WriteFile(gitignoreAbs, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil { //nolint:gosec // path is from AbsPath or constant
   720→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write gitignore: %w", err)
   721→	}
   722→	return nil
   723→}
   724→
   725→// checkCanRewind checks if working directory is clean enough for rewind.
   726→// Returns (canRewind, reason, error). Shared by shadow and linear-shadow strategies.
   727→func checkCanRewind() (bool, string, error) {
   728→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   729→	if err != nil {
   730→		return false, "", fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   731→	}
   732→
   733→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   734→	if err != nil {
   735→		return false, "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree: %w", err)
   736→	}
   737→
   738→	status, err := worktree.Status()
   739→	if err != nil {
   740→		return false, "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get status: %w", err)
   741→	}
   742→
   743→	if status.IsClean() {
   744→		return true, "", nil
   745→	}
   746→
   747→	var modified, added, deleted []string
   748→	for file, st := range status {
   749→		// Skip .entire directory
   750→		if paths.IsInfrastructurePath(file) {
   751→			continue
   752→		}
   753→
   754→		// Skip untracked files
   755→		if st.Worktree == git.Untracked {
   756→			continue
   757→		}
   758→
   759→		switch {
   760→		case st.Staging == git.Added || st.Worktree == git.Added:
   761→			added = append(added, file)
   762→		case st.Staging == git.Deleted || st.Worktree == git.Deleted:
   763→			deleted = append(deleted, file)
   764→		case st.Staging == git.Modified || st.Worktree == git.Modified:
   765→			modified = append(modified, file)
   766→		}
   767→	}
   768→
   769→	if len(modified) == 0 && len(added) == 0 && len(deleted) == 0 {
   770→		return true, "", nil
   771→	}
   772→
   773→	var msg strings.Builder
   774→	msg.WriteString("You have uncommitted changes:\n")
   775→	for _, f := range modified {
   776→		msg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("  modified:   %s\n", f))
   777→	}
   778→	for _, f := range added {
   779→		msg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("  added:      %s\n", f))
   780→	}
   781→	for _, f := range deleted {
   782→		msg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("  deleted:    %s\n", f))
   783→	}
   784→	msg.WriteString("\nPlease commit or stash your changes before rewinding.")
   785→
   786→	return false, msg.String(), nil
   787→}
   788→
   789→// checkCanRewindWithWarning checks working directory and returns a warning with diff stats.
   790→// Unlike checkCanRewind, this always returns canRewind=true but includes a warning message
   791→// with +/- line stats for uncommitted changes. Used by manual-commit strategy.
   792→func checkCanRewindWithWarning() (bool, string, error) {
   793→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   794→	if err != nil {
   795→		// Can't open repo - still allow rewind but without stats
   796→		return true, "", nil //nolint:nilerr // Rewind allowed even if repo can't be opened
   797→	}
   798→
   799→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   800→	if err != nil {
   801→		return true, "", nil //nolint:nilerr // Rewind allowed even if worktree can't be accessed
   802→	}
   803→
   804→	status, err := worktree.Status()
   805→	if err != nil {
   806→		return true, "", nil //nolint:nilerr // Rewind allowed even if status can't be retrieved
   807→	}
   808→
   809→	if status.IsClean() {
   810→		return true, "", nil
   811→	}
   812→
   813→	// Get HEAD commit tree for comparison - if we can't get it, just return without stats
   814→	head, err := repo.Head()
   815→	if err != nil {
   816→		return true, "", nil //nolint:nilerr // Rewind allowed even without HEAD (e.g., empty repo)
   817→	}
   818→
   819→	headCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   820→	if err != nil {
   821→		return true, "", nil //nolint:nilerr // Rewind allowed even if commit lookup fails
   822→	}
   823→
   824→	headTree, err := headCommit.Tree()
   825→	if err != nil {
   826→		return true, "", nil //nolint:nilerr // Rewind allowed even if tree lookup fails
   827→	}
   828→
   829→	type fileChange struct {
   830→		status   string // "modified", "added", "deleted"
   831→		added    int
   832→		removed  int
   833→		filename string
   834→	}
   835→
   836→	var changes []fileChange
   837→	// Use repo root, not cwd - git status returns paths relative to repo root
   838→	repoRoot, err := GetWorktreePath()
   839→	if err != nil {
   840→		return true, "", nil //nolint:nilerr // Rewind allowed even if repo root lookup fails
   841→	}
   842→
   843→	for file, st := range status {
   844→		// Skip .entire directory
   845→		if paths.IsInfrastructurePath(file) {
   846→			continue
   847→		}
   848→
   849→		// Skip untracked files
   850→		if st.Worktree == git.Untracked {
   851→			continue
   852→		}
   853→
   854→		var change fileChange
   855→		change.filename = file
   856→
   857→		switch {
   858→		case st.Staging == git.Added || st.Worktree == git.Added:
   859→			change.status = "added"
   860→			// New file - count all lines as added
   861→			absPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, file)
   862→			if content, err := os.ReadFile(absPath); err == nil { //nolint:gosec // absPath is repo root + relative path from git status
   863→				change.added = countLines(content)
   864→			}
   865→		case st.Staging == git.Deleted || st.Worktree == git.Deleted:
   866→			change.status = "deleted"
   867→			// Deleted file - count lines from HEAD as removed
   868→			if entry, err := headTree.File(file); err == nil {
   869→				if content, err := entry.Contents(); err == nil {
   870→					change.removed = countLines([]byte(content))
   871→				}
   872→			}
   873→		case st.Staging == git.Modified || st.Worktree == git.Modified:
   874→			change.status = "modified"
   875→			// Modified file - compute diff stats
   876→			var headContent, workContent []byte
   877→			if entry, err := headTree.File(file); err == nil {
   878→				if content, err := entry.Contents(); err == nil {
   879→					headContent = []byte(content)
   880→				}
   881→			}
   882→			absPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, file)
   883→			if content, err := os.ReadFile(absPath); err == nil { //nolint:gosec // absPath is repo root + relative path from git status
   884→				workContent = content
   885→			}
   886→			if headContent != nil && workContent != nil {
   887→				change.added, change.removed = computeDiffStats(headContent, workContent)
   888→			}
   889→		default:
   890→			continue
   891→		}
   892→
   893→		changes = append(changes, change)
   894→	}
   895→
   896→	if len(changes) == 0 {
   897→		return true, "", nil
   898→	}
   899→
   900→	// Sort changes by filename for consistent output
   901→	sort.Slice(changes, func(i, j int) bool {
   902→		return changes[i].filename < changes[j].filename
   903→	})
   904→
   905→	var msg strings.Builder
   906→	msg.WriteString("The following uncommitted changes will be reverted:\n")
   907→
   908→	totalAdded, totalRemoved := 0, 0
   909→	for _, c := range changes {
   910→		totalAdded += c.added
   911→		totalRemoved += c.removed
   912→
   913→		var stats string
   914→		switch {
   915→		case c.added > 0 && c.removed > 0:
   916→			stats = fmt.Sprintf("+%d/-%d", c.added, c.removed)
   917→		case c.added > 0:
   918→			stats = fmt.Sprintf("+%d", c.added)
   919→		case c.removed > 0:
   920→			stats = fmt.Sprintf("-%d", c.removed)
   921→		}
   922→
   923→		msg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("  %-10s %s", c.status+":", c.filename))
   924→		if stats != "" {
   925→			msg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(" (%s)", stats))
   926→		}
   927→		msg.WriteString("\n")
   928→	}
   929→
   930→	if totalAdded > 0 || totalRemoved > 0 {
   931→		msg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("\nTotal: +%d/-%d lines\n", totalAdded, totalRemoved))
   932→	}
   933→
   934→	return true, msg.String(), nil
   935→}
   936→
   937→// countLines counts the number of lines in content.
   938→func countLines(content []byte) int {
   939→	if len(content) == 0 {
   940→		return 0
   941→	}
   942→	count := 1
   943→	for _, b := range content {
   944→		if b == '\n' {
   945→			count++
   946→		}
   947→	}
   948→	// Don't count trailing newline as extra line
   949→	if len(content) > 0 && content[len(content)-1] == '\n' {
   950→		count--
   951→	}
   952→	return count
   953→}
   954→
   955→// computeDiffStats computes added and removed line counts between old and new content.
   956→// Uses a simple line-based diff algorithm.
   957→func computeDiffStats(oldContent, newContent []byte) (added, removed int) {
   958→	oldLines := splitLines(oldContent)
   959→	newLines := splitLines(newContent)
   960→
   961→	// Build a set of old lines with counts
   962→	oldSet := make(map[string]int)
   963→	for _, line := range oldLines {
   964→		oldSet[line]++
   965→	}
   966→
   967→	// Check which new lines are truly new
   968→	for _, line := range newLines {
   969→		if oldSet[line] > 0 {
   970→			oldSet[line]--
   971→		} else {
   972→			added++
   973→		}
   974→	}
   975→
   976→	// Remaining old lines are removed
   977→	for _, count := range oldSet {
   978→		removed += count
   979→	}
   980→
   981→	return added, removed
   982→}
   983→
   984→// splitLines splits content into lines, preserving empty lines.
   985→func splitLines(content []byte) []string {
   986→	if len(content) == 0 {
   987→		return nil
   988→	}
   989→	s := string(content)
   990→	// Remove trailing newline to avoid empty last element
   991→	s = strings.TrimSuffix(s, "\n")
   992→	return strings.Split(s, "\n")
   993→}
   994→
   995→// fileExists checks if a file exists at the given path.
   996→func fileExists(path string) bool {
   997→	_, err := os.Stat(path)
   998→	return err == nil
   999→}
  1000→
  1001→// StageFilesContext describes what type of staging operation this is (for messages).
  1002→type StageFilesContext string
  1003→
  1004→const (
  1005→	// StageForSession is used when staging files for a session checkpoint.
  1006→	StageForSession StageFilesContext = "session"
  1007→	// StageForTask is used when staging files for a task checkpoint.
  1008→	StageForTask StageFilesContext = "task"
  1009→)
  1010→
  1011→// StageFiles stages modified, new, and deleted files to the git worktree.
  1012→//
  1013→// This function handles three categories of file changes:
  1014→//  1. Modified files: existing files that have been changed
  1015→//  2. New files: files that were created during the session
  1016→//  3. Deleted files: files that were removed during the session
  1017→//
  1018→// Error Handling Strategy:
  1019→//   - Individual file staging errors are logged to stderr but don't fail the operation
  1020→//   - This ensures that partial staging succeeds even if some files have issues
  1021→//   - If a modified file no longer exists, it's treated as a deletion
  1022→//
  1023→// The stageCtx parameter is used for user-facing messages to indicate whether
  1024→// this is staging for a session checkpoint or a task checkpoint.
  1025→func StageFiles(worktree *git.Worktree, modified, newFiles, deleted []string, stageCtx StageFilesContext) {
  1026→	// Get repo root for resolving file paths
  1027→	// This is critical because fileExists() uses os.Stat() which resolves relative to CWD,
  1028→	// but worktree.Add/Remove resolve relative to repo root. If CWD != repo root,
  1029→	// fileExists() could return false for existing files, causing worktree.Remove()
  1030→	// to incorrectly delete them.
  1031→	repoRoot := worktree.Filesystem.Root()
  1032→
  1033→	// Stage modified files
  1034→	for _, file := range modified {
  1035→		// Resolve path relative to repo root for existence check
  1036→		absPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, file)
  1037→		if fileExists(absPath) {
  1038→			if _, err := worktree.Add(file); err != nil {
  1039→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Failed to stage %s: %v\n", file, err)
  1040→			} else {
  1041→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Staged: %s\n", file)
  1042→			}
  1043→		} else {
  1044→			// File was deleted - stage the deletion
  1045→			if _, err := worktree.Remove(file); err != nil {
  1046→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  File not found or deleted: %s\n", file)
  1047→			}
  1048→		}
  1049→	}
  1050→
  1051→	// Stage new files
  1052→	if len(newFiles) > 0 {
  1053→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Staging %d new files created during %s:\n", len(newFiles), stageCtx)
  1054→		for _, file := range newFiles {
  1055→			if _, err := worktree.Add(file); err != nil {
  1056→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Failed to stage %s: %v\n", file, err)
  1057→			} else {
  1058→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Staged new file: %s\n", file)
  1059→			}
  1060→		}
  1061→	}
  1062→
  1063→	// Stage deleted files
  1064→	for _, file := range deleted {
  1065→		if _, err := worktree.Remove(file); err != nil {
  1066→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Failed to stage deleted file %s: %v\n", file, err)
  1067→		} else {
  1068→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Staged deleted file: %s\n", file)
  1069→		}
  1070→	}
  1071→}
  1072→
  1073→// getTaskCheckpointFromTree retrieves a task checkpoint from a commit tree.
  1074→// Shared implementation for shadow and linear-shadow strategies.
  1075→func getTaskCheckpointFromTree(point RewindPoint) (*TaskCheckpoint, error) {
  1076→	if !point.IsTaskCheckpoint {
  1077→		return nil, ErrNotTaskCheckpoint
  1078→	}
  1079→
  1080→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
  1081→	if err != nil {
  1082→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
  1083→	}
  1084→
  1085→	commitHash := plumbing.NewHash(point.ID)
  1086→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(commitHash)
  1087→	if err != nil {
  1088→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit: %w", err)
  1089→	}
  1090→
  1091→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
  1092→	if err != nil {
  1093→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get tree: %w", err)
  1094→	}
  1095→
  1096→	// Read checkpoint.json from the tree
  1097→	checkpointPath := point.MetadataDir + "/checkpoint.json"
  1098→	file, err := tree.File(checkpointPath)
  1099→	if err != nil {
  1100→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to find checkpoint at %s: %w", checkpointPath, err)
  1101→	}
  1102→
  1103→	content, err := file.Contents()
  1104→	if err != nil {
  1105→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
  1106→	}
  1107→
  1108→	var checkpoint TaskCheckpoint
  1109→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &checkpoint); err != nil {
  1110→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse checkpoint: %w", err)
  1111→	}
  1112→
  1113→	return &checkpoint, nil
  1114→}
  1115→
  1116→// getTaskTranscriptFromTree retrieves a task transcript from a commit tree.
  1117→// Shared implementation for shadow and linear-shadow strategies.
  1118→func getTaskTranscriptFromTree(point RewindPoint) ([]byte, error) {
  1119→	if !point.IsTaskCheckpoint {
  1120→		return nil, ErrNotTaskCheckpoint
  1121→	}
  1122→
  1123→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
  1124→	if err != nil {
  1125→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
  1126→	}
  1127→
  1128→	commitHash := plumbing.NewHash(point.ID)
  1129→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(commitHash)
  1130→	if err != nil {
  1131→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit: %w", err)
  1132→	}
  1133→
  1134→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
  1135→	if err != nil {
  1136→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get tree: %w", err)
  1137→	}
  1138→
  1139→	// MetadataDir format: .entire/metadata/<session>/tasks/<toolUseID>
  1140→	// Session transcript is at: .entire/metadata/<session>/<TranscriptFileName>
  1141→	sessionDir := filepath.Dir(filepath.Dir(point.MetadataDir))
  1142→
  1143→	// Try current format first, then legacy
  1144→	transcriptPath := sessionDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileName
  1145→	file, err := tree.File(transcriptPath)
  1146→	if err != nil {
  1147→		transcriptPath = sessionDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy
  1148→		file, err = tree.File(transcriptPath)
  1149→		if err != nil {
  1150→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to find transcript: %w", err)
  1151→		}
  1152→	}
  1153→
  1154→	content, err := file.Contents()
  1155→	if err != nil {
  1156→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
  1157→	}
  1158→
  1159→	return []byte(content), nil
  1160→}
  1161→
  1162→// ErrBranchNotFound is returned by DeleteBranchCLI when the branch does not exist.
  1163→var ErrBranchNotFound = errors.New("branch not found")
  1164→
  1165→// DeleteBranchCLI deletes a git branch using the git CLI.
  1166→// Uses `git branch -D` instead of go-git's RemoveReference because go-git v5
  1167→// doesn't properly persist deletions when refs are packed (.git/packed-refs)
  1168→// or in a worktree context. This is the same class of go-git v5 bug that
  1169→// affects checkout and reset --hard (see HardResetWithProtection).
  1170→//
  1171→// Returns ErrBranchNotFound if the branch does not exist, allowing callers
  1172→// to use errors.Is for idempotent deletion patterns.
  1173→func DeleteBranchCLI(branchName string) error {
  1174→	ctx := context.Background()
  1175→
  1176→	// Pre-check: verify the branch exists so callers get a structured error
  1177→	// instead of parsing git's output string (which varies across locales).
  1178→	// git show-ref exits 1 for "not found" and 128+ for fatal errors (corrupt
  1179→	// repo, permissions, not a git directory). Only map exit code 1 to
  1180→	// ErrBranchNotFound; propagate other failures as-is.
  1181→	check := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "show-ref", "--verify", "--quiet", "refs/heads/"+branchName) //nolint:gosec // branchName comes from internal shadow branch naming
  1182→	if err := check.Run(); err != nil {
  1183→		var exitErr *exec.ExitError
  1184→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) && exitErr.ExitCode() == 1 {
  1185→			return fmt.Errorf("%w: %s", ErrBranchNotFound, branchName)
  1186→		}
  1187→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to check branch %s: %w", branchName, err)
  1188→	}
  1189→
  1190→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "branch", "-D", "--", branchName)
  1191→	if output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
  1192→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to delete branch %s: %s: %w", branchName, strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), err)
  1193→	}
  1194→	return nil
  1195→}
  1196→
  1197→// branchExistsCLI checks if a branch exists using git CLI.
  1198→// Returns nil if the branch exists, or an error if it does not.
  1199→func branchExistsCLI(branchName string) error {
  1200→	ctx := context.Background()
  1201→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "show-ref", "--verify", "--quiet", "refs/heads/"+branchName) //nolint:gosec // branchName comes from internal shadow branch naming
  1202→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
  1203→		return fmt.Errorf("branch %s not found: %w", branchName, err)
  1204→	}
  1205→	return nil
  1206→}
  1207→
  1208→// HardResetWithProtection performs a git reset --hard to the specified commit.
  1209→// Uses the git CLI instead of go-git because go-git's HardReset incorrectly
  1210→// deletes untracked directories (like .entire/) even when they're in .gitignore.
  1211→// Returns the short commit ID (7 chars) on success for display purposes.
  1212→func HardResetWithProtection(commitHash plumbing.Hash) (shortID string, err error) {
  1213→	ctx := context.Background()
  1214→	hashStr := commitHash.String()
  1215→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "reset", "--hard", hashStr) //nolint:gosec // hashStr is a plumbing.Hash, not user input
  1216→	if output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
  1217→		return "", fmt.Errorf("reset failed: %s: %w", strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), err)
  1218→	}
  1219→
  1220→	// Return short commit ID for display
  1221→	shortID = hashStr
  1222→	if len(shortID) > 7 {
  1223→		shortID = shortID[:7]
  1224→	}
  1225→	return shortID, nil
  1226→}
  1227→
  1228→// collectUntrackedFiles collects untracked files in the working directory that are
  1229→// NOT ignored by .gitignore. This is used to capture the initial state when starting
  1230→// a session, ensuring untracked files present at session start are preserved during rewind.
  1231→// Uses "git ls-files --others --exclude-standard -z" to respect .gitignore rules,
  1232→// avoiding bloated session state from large ignored directories like node_modules/.
  1233→// Returns paths relative to the repository root.
  1234→func collectUntrackedFiles() ([]string, error) {
  1235→	repoRoot, err := GetWorktreePath()
  1236→	if err != nil {
  1237→		repoRoot = "."
  1238→	}
  1239→
  1240→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), "git", "ls-files", "--others", "--exclude-standard", "-z")
  1241→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
  1242→	output, err := cmd.Output()
  1243→	if err != nil {
  1244→		var exitErr *exec.ExitError
  1245→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
  1246→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("git ls-files failed: %s: %w", strings.TrimSpace(string(exitErr.Stderr)), err)
  1247→		}
  1248→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("git ls-files failed: %w", err)
  1249→	}
  1250→
  1251→	raw := string(output)
  1252→	if raw == "" {
  1253→		return nil, nil
  1254→	}
  1255→
  1256→	var files []string
  1257→	for _, f := range strings.Split(raw, "\x00") {
  1258→		// Defense-in-depth: filter protected paths even though --exclude-standard should already handle them
  1259→		if f != "" && !isProtectedPath(f) {
  1260→			files = append(files, f)
  1261→		}
  1262→	}
  1263→	return files, nil
  1264→}
  1265→
  1266→// ExtractSessionIDFromCommit extracts the session ID from a commit's trailers.
  1267→// It checks the Entire-Session trailer first, then falls back to extracting from
  1268→// the metadata directory path in the Entire-Metadata trailer.
  1269→// Returns empty string if no session ID is found.
  1270→func ExtractSessionIDFromCommit(commit *object.Commit) string {
  1271→	// Try Entire-Session trailer first
  1272→	if sessionID, found := trailers.ParseSession(commit.Message); found {
  1273→		return sessionID
  1274→	}
  1275→
  1276→	// Try extracting from metadata directory (last path component)
  1277→	if metadataDir, found := trailers.ParseMetadata(commit.Message); found {
  1278→		return filepath.Base(metadataDir)
  1279→	}
  1280→
  1281→	return ""
  1282→}
  1283→
  1284→// NOTE: The following git tree helper functions have been moved to checkpoint/ package:
  1285→// - FlattenTree -> checkpoint.FlattenTree
  1286→// - CreateBlobFromContent -> checkpoint.CreateBlobFromContent
  1287→// - BuildTreeFromEntries -> checkpoint.BuildTreeFromEntries
  1288→// - sortTreeEntries (internal to checkpoint package)
  1289→// - treeNode, insertIntoTree, buildTreeObject (internal to checkpoint package)
  1290→//
  1291→// See push_common.go and session_test.go for usage examples.
  1292→
  1293→// createCommit creates a commit object
  1294→func createCommit(repo *git.Repository, treeHash, parentHash plumbing.Hash, message, authorName, authorEmail string) (plumbing.Hash, error) { //nolint:unparam // already present in codebase
  1295→	now := time.Now()
  1296→	sig := object.Signature{
  1297→		Name:  authorName,
  1298→		Email: authorEmail,
  1299→		When:  now,
  1300→	}
  1301→
  1302→	commit := &object.Commit{
  1303→		TreeHash:  treeHash,
  1304→		Author:    sig,
  1305→		Committer: sig,
  1306→		Message:   message,
  1307→	}
  1308→
  1309→	// Add parent if not a new branch
  1310→	if parentHash != plumbing.ZeroHash {
  1311→		commit.ParentHashes = []plumbing.Hash{parentHash}
  1312→	}
  1313→
  1314→	obj := repo.Storer.NewEncodedObject()
  1315→	if err := commit.Encode(obj); err != nil {
  1316→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to encode commit: %w", err)
  1317→	}
  1318→
  1319→	hash, err := repo.Storer.SetEncodedObject(obj)
  1320→	if err != nil {
  1321→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to store commit: %w", err)
  1322→	}
  1323→
  1324→	return hash, nil
  1325→}
  1326→
  1327→// getSessionDescriptionFromTree reads the first line of prompt.txt or context.md from a git tree.
  1328→// This is the tree-based equivalent of getSessionDescription (which reads from filesystem).
  1329→//
  1330→// If metadataDir is provided, looks for files at metadataDir/prompt.txt or metadataDir/context.md.
  1331→// If metadataDir is empty, first tries the root of the tree (for when the tree is already
  1332→// the session directory, e.g., auto-commit strategy's sharded metadata), then falls back to
  1333→// searching for .entire/metadata/*/prompt.txt or context.md (for full worktree trees).
  1334→func getSessionDescriptionFromTree(tree *object.Tree, metadataDir string) string {
  1335→	// Helper to read first line from a file in tree
  1336→	readFirstLine := func(path string) string {
  1337→		file, err := tree.File(path)
  1338→		if err != nil {
  1339→			return ""
  1340→		}
  1341→		content, err := file.Contents()
  1342→		if err != nil {
  1343→			return ""
  1344→		}
  1345→		lines := strings.SplitN(content, "\n", 2)
  1346→		if len(lines) > 0 && lines[0] != "" {
  1347→			desc := strings.TrimSpace(lines[0])
  1348→			// Remove markdown header prefix if present
  1349→			return strings.TrimPrefix(desc, "# ")
  1350→		}
  1351→		return ""
  1352→	}
  1353→
  1354→	// If metadataDir is provided, look there directly
  1355→	if metadataDir != "" {
  1356→		if desc := readFirstLine(metadataDir + "/" + paths.PromptFileName); desc != "" {
  1357→			return desc
  1358→		}
  1359→		if desc := readFirstLine(metadataDir + "/" + paths.ContextFileName); desc != "" {
  1360→			return desc
  1361→		}
  1362→		return NoDescription
  1363→	}
  1364→
  1365→	// No metadataDir provided - first try looking at the root of the tree
  1366→	// (used when the tree is already the session directory)
  1367→	if desc := readFirstLine(paths.PromptFileName); desc != "" {
  1368→		return desc
  1369→	}
  1370→	if desc := readFirstLine(paths.ContextFileName); desc != "" {
  1371→		return desc
  1372→	}
  1373→
  1374→	// Fall back to searching for .entire/metadata/*/prompt.txt or context.md
  1375→	// (used when the tree is the full worktree)
  1376→	var desc string
  1377→	//nolint:errcheck // We ignore errors here as we're just searching for a description
  1378→	_ = tree.Files().ForEach(func(f *object.File) error {
  1379→		if desc != "" {
  1380→			return nil // Already found description
  1381→		}
  1382→		name := f.Name
  1383→		if strings.Contains(name, ".entire/metadata/") {
  1384→			if strings.HasSuffix(name, "/"+paths.PromptFileName) || strings.HasSuffix(name, "/"+paths.ContextFileName) {
  1385→				content, err := f.Contents()
  1386→				if err != nil {
  1387→					return nil //nolint:nilerr // Skip files we can't read, continue searching
  1388→				}
  1389→				lines := strings.SplitN(content, "\n", 2)
  1390→				if len(lines) > 0 && lines[0] != "" {
  1391→					desc = strings.TrimSpace(lines[0])
  1392→					desc = strings.TrimPrefix(desc, "# ")
  1393→				}
  1394→			}
  1395→		}
  1396→		return nil
  1397→	})
  1398→
  1399→	if desc != "" {
  1400→		return desc
  1401→	}
  1402→	return NoDescription
  1403→}
  1404→
  1405→// GetGitAuthorFromRepo retrieves the git user.name and user.email,
  1406→// checking both the repository-local config and the global ~/.gitconfig.
  1407→// Delegates to checkpoint.GetGitAuthorFromRepo — this wrapper exists so
  1408→// callers within the strategy package don't need a qualified import.
  1409→func GetGitAuthorFromRepo(repo *git.Repository) (name, email string) {
  1410→	return checkpoint.GetGitAuthorFromRepo(repo)
  1411→}
  1412→
  1413→// GetCurrentBranchName returns the short name of the current branch if HEAD points to a branch.
  1414→// Returns an empty string if in detached HEAD state or if there's an error reading HEAD.
  1415→// This is used to capture branch metadata for checkpoints.
  1416→func GetCurrentBranchName(repo *git.Repository) string {
  1417→	head, err := repo.Head()
  1418→	if err != nil || !head.Name().IsBranch() {
  1419→		return ""
  1420→	}
  1421→	return head.Name().Short()
  1422→}
  1423→
  1424→// getMainBranchHash returns the hash of the main branch (main or master).
  1425→// Returns ZeroHash if no main branch is found.
  1426→func GetMainBranchHash(repo *git.Repository) plumbing.Hash {
  1427→	// Try common main branch names
  1428→	for _, branchName := range []string{branchMain, branchMaster} {
  1429→		// Try local branch first
  1430→		ref, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branchName), true)
  1431→		if err == nil {
  1432→			return ref.Hash()
  1433→		}
  1434→		// Try remote tracking branch
  1435→		ref, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", branchName), true)
  1436→		if err == nil {
  1437→			return ref.Hash()
  1438→		}
  1439→	}
  1440→	return plumbing.ZeroHash
  1441→}
  1442→
  1443→// GetDefaultBranchName returns the name of the default branch.
  1444→// First checks origin/HEAD, then falls back to checking if main/master exists.
  1445→// Returns empty string if unable to determine.
  1446→// NOTE: Duplicated from cli/git_operations.go - see ENT-129 for consolidation.
  1447→func GetDefaultBranchName(repo *git.Repository) string {
  1448→	// Try to get the symbolic reference for origin/HEAD
  1449→	// Use resolved=false to get the symbolic ref itself, then extract its target
  1450→	ref, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", "HEAD"), false)
  1451→	if err == nil && ref != nil && ref.Type() == plumbing.SymbolicReference {
  1452→		target := ref.Target().String()
  1453→		if branchName, found := strings.CutPrefix(target, "refs/remotes/origin/"); found {
  1454→			return branchName
  1455→		}
  1456→	}
  1457→
  1458→	// Fallback: check if origin/main or origin/master exists
  1459→	if _, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", branchMain), true); err == nil {
  1460→		return branchMain
  1461→	}
  1462→	if _, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", branchMaster), true); err == nil {
  1463→		return branchMaster
  1464→	}
  1465→
  1466→	// Final fallback: check local branches
  1467→	if _, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branchMain), true); err == nil {
  1468→		return branchMain
  1469→	}
  1470→	if _, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branchMaster), true); err == nil {
  1471→		return branchMaster
  1472→	}
  1473→
  1474→	return ""
  1475→}
  1476→
  1477→// IsOnDefaultBranch checks if the repository HEAD is on the default branch.
  1478→// Returns (isOnDefault, currentBranchName).
  1479→// NOTE: Duplicated from cli/git_operations.go - see ENT-129 for consolidation.
  1480→func IsOnDefaultBranch(repo *git.Repository) (bool, string) {
  1481→	currentBranch := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)
  1482→	if currentBranch == "" {
  1483→		return false, ""
  1484→	}
  1485→
  1486→	defaultBranch := GetDefaultBranchName(repo)
  1487→	if defaultBranch == "" {
  1488→		// Can't determine default, check common names
  1489→		if currentBranch == branchMain || currentBranch == branchMaster {
  1490→			return true, currentBranch
  1491→		}
  1492→		return false, currentBranch
  1493→	}
  1494→
  1495→	return currentBranch == defaultBranch, currentBranch
  1496→}
  1497→

   184→// runEnableWithStrategy enables Entire with a specified strategy (non-interactive).
   185→// The selectedStrategy can be either a display name (manual-commit, auto-commit)
   186→// or an internal name (manual-commit, auto-commit).
   187→// agents must be provided by the caller (via detectOrSelectAgent).
   188→func runEnableWithStrategy(w io.Writer, agents []agent.Agent, selectedStrategy string, localDev, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry bool) error {
   189→	// Map the strategy to internal name if it's a display name
   190→	internalStrategy := selectedStrategy
   191→	if mapped, ok := strategyDisplayToInternal[selectedStrategy]; ok {
   192→		internalStrategy = mapped
   193→	}
   194→
   195→	// Validate the strategy exists
   196→	strat, err := strategy.Get(internalStrategy)
   197→	if err != nil {
   198→		return fmt.Errorf("unknown strategy: %s (use manual-commit or auto-commit)", selectedStrategy)
   199→	}
   200→
   201→	// Uninstall hooks for agents that were previously active but are no longer selected
   202→	if err := uninstallDeselectedAgentHooks(w, agents); err != nil {
   203→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to clean up deselected agents: %w", err)
   204→	}
   205→
   206→	// Setup agent hooks for all selected agents
   207→	for _, ag := range agents {
   208→		if _, err := setupAgentHooks(ag, localDev, forceHooks); err != nil {
   209→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup %s hooks: %w", ag.Type(), err)
   210→		}
   211→	}
   212→
   213→	// Setup .entire directory
   214→	if _, err := setupEntireDirectory(); err != nil {
   215→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup .entire directory: %w", err)
   216→	}
   217→
   218→	// Load existing settings to preserve other options (like strategy_options.push)
   219→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   220→	if err != nil {
   221→		// If we can't load, start with defaults
   222→		settings = &EntireSettings{}
   223→	}
   224→	// Update the specific fields
   225→	settings.Strategy = internalStrategy
   226→	settings.LocalDev = localDev
   227→	settings.Enabled = true
   228→
   229→	// Set push_sessions option if --skip-push-sessions flag was provided
   230→	if skipPushSessions {
   231→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   232→			settings.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]interface{})
   233→		}
   234→		settings.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"] = false
   235→	}
   236→
   237→	// Handle telemetry for non-interactive mode
   238→	// Note: if telemetry is nil (not configured), it defaults to disabled
   239→	if !telemetry || os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT") != "" {
   240→		f := false
   241→		settings.Telemetry = &f
   242→	}
   243→
   244→	// Determine which settings file to write to
   245→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
   246→	if err != nil {
   247→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir // Fallback to relative
   248→	}
   249→	shouldUseLocal, showNotification := determineSettingsTarget(entireDirAbs, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings)
   250→
   251→	if showNotification {
   252→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "Info: Project settings exist. Saving to settings.local.json instead.")
   253→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Use --project to update the project settings file.")
   254→	}
   255→
   256→	configDisplay := configDisplayProject
   257→	if shouldUseLocal {
   258→		if err := SaveEntireSettingsLocal(settings); err != nil {
   259→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save local settings: %w", err)
   260→		}
   261→		configDisplay = configDisplayLocal
   262→	} else {
   263→		if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   264→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   265→		}
   266→	}
   267→
   268→	// Install git hooks AFTER saving settings (InstallGitHook reads local_dev from settings)
   269→	if _, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(true); err != nil {
   270→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
   271→	}
   272→	strategy.CheckAndWarnHookManagers(w)
   273→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Hooks installed")
   274→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Project configured (%s)\n", configDisplay)
   275→
   276→	// Let the strategy handle its own setup requirements
   277→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   278→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup strategy: %w", err)
   279→	}
   280→
   281→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nReady.")
   282→
   283→	return nil
   284→}
   285→
   286→// runEnableInteractive runs the interactive enable flow.
   287→// agents must be provided by the caller (via detectOrSelectAgent).
   288→func runEnableInteractive(w io.Writer, agents []agent.Agent, localDev, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry bool) error {
   289→	// Uninstall hooks for agents that were previously active but are no longer selected
   290→	if err := uninstallDeselectedAgentHooks(w, agents); err != nil {
   291→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to clean up deselected agents: %w", err)
   292→	}
   293→
   294→	// Setup agent hooks for all selected agents
   295→	for _, ag := range agents {
   296→		if _, err := setupAgentHooks(ag, localDev, forceHooks); err != nil {
   297→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup %s hooks: %w", ag.Type(), err)
   298→		}
   299→	}
   300→
   301→	// Setup .entire directory
   302→	if _, err := setupEntireDirectory(); err != nil {
   303→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup .entire directory: %w", err)
   304→	}
   305→
   306→	// Use the default strategy (manual-commit)
   307→	internalStrategy := strategy.DefaultStrategyName
   308→
   309→	// Load existing settings to preserve other options (like strategy_options.push)
   310→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   311→	if err != nil {
   312→		// If we can't load, start with defaults
   313→		settings = &EntireSettings{}
   314→	}
   315→	// Update the specific fields
   316→	settings.Strategy = internalStrategy
   317→	settings.LocalDev = localDev
   318→	settings.Enabled = true
   319→
   320→	// Set push_sessions option if --skip-push-sessions flag was provided
   321→	if skipPushSessions {
   322→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   323→			settings.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]interface{})
   324→		}
   325→		settings.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"] = false
   326→	}
   327→
   328→	// Determine which settings file to write to
   329→	// First run always creates settings.json (no prompt)
   330→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
   331→	if err != nil {
   332→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir // Fallback to relative
   333→	}
   334→	shouldUseLocal, showNotification := determineSettingsTarget(entireDirAbs, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings)
   335→
   336→	if showNotification {
   337→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "Info: Project settings exist. Saving to settings.local.json instead.")
   338→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Use --project to update the project settings file.")
   339→	}
   340→
   341→	// Helper to save settings to the appropriate file
   342→	saveSettings := func() error {
   343→		if shouldUseLocal {
   344→			return SaveEntireSettingsLocal(settings)
   345→		}
   346→		return SaveEntireSettings(settings)
   347→	}
   348→
   349→	// Save settings before telemetry prompt so config is persisted even if the user cancels
   350→	if err := saveSettings(); err != nil {
   351→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   352→	}
   353→
   354→	// Install git hooks AFTER saving settings (InstallGitHook reads local_dev from settings)
   355→	if _, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(true); err != nil {
   356→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
   357→	}
   358→	strategy.CheckAndWarnHookManagers(w)
   359→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Hooks installed")
   360→
   361→	configDisplay := configDisplayProject
   362→	if shouldUseLocal {
   363→		configDisplay = configDisplayLocal
   364→	}
   365→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Project configured (%s)\n", configDisplay)
   366→
   367→	// Ask about telemetry consent (only if not already asked)
   368→	fmt.Fprintln(w)
   369→	if err := promptTelemetryConsent(settings, telemetry); err != nil {
   370→		return fmt.Errorf("telemetry consent: %w", err)
   371→	}
   372→	// Save again to persist telemetry choice
   373→	if err := saveSettings(); err != nil {
   374→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   375→	}
   376→
   377→	// Let the strategy handle its own setup requirements
   378→	strat, err := strategy.Get(internalStrategy)
   379→	if err != nil {
   380→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get strategy: %w", err)
   381→	}
   382→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   383→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup strategy: %w", err)
   384→	}
   385→
   386→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nReady.")
   387→
   388→	return nil
   389→}
   390→
   391→// runEnable is a simple enable that just sets the enabled flag (for programmatic use).
   392→func runEnable(w io.Writer) error {
   393→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   394→	if err != nil {
   395→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
   396→	}
   397→
   398→	settings.Enabled = true
   399→	if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   400→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   401→	}
   402→
   403→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Entire is now enabled.")
   404→	return nil
   405→}
   406→
   407→func runDisable(w io.Writer, useProjectSettings bool) error {
   408→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   409→	if err != nil {
   410→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
   411→	}
   412→
   413→	settings.Enabled = false
   414→
   415→	// If --project flag is specified, always write to project settings
   416→	if useProjectSettings {
   417→		if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   418→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   419→		}
   420→	} else {
   421→		// Always write to local settings file (create if doesn't exist)
   422→		if err := SaveEntireSettingsLocal(settings); err != nil {
   423→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save local settings: %w", err)
   424→		}
   425→	}
   426→
   427→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Entire is now disabled.")
   428→	return nil
   429→}
   430→
   431→// DisabledMessage is the message shown when Entire is disabled
   432→const DisabledMessage = "Entire is disabled. Run `entire enable` to re-enable."
   433→
   434→// checkDisabledGuard checks if Entire is disabled and prints a message if so.
   435→// Returns true if the caller should exit (i.e., Entire is disabled).
   436→// On error reading settings, defaults to enabled (returns false).
   437→func checkDisabledGuard(w io.Writer) bool {
   438→	enabled, err := IsEnabled()
   439→	if err != nil {
   440→		// Default to enabled on error
   441→		return false
   442→	}
   443→	if !enabled {
   444→		fmt.Fprintln(w, DisabledMessage)
   445→		return true
   446→	}
   447→	return false
   448→}
   449→
   450→// uninstallDeselectedAgentHooks removes hooks for agents that were previously
   451→// installed but are not in the selected list. This handles the case where a user
   452→// re-runs `entire enable` and deselects an agent.
   453→func uninstallDeselectedAgentHooks(w io.Writer, selectedAgents []agent.Agent) error {
   454→	installedNames := GetAgentsWithHooksInstalled()
   455→	if len(installedNames) == 0 {
   456→		return nil
   457→	}
   458→
   459→	selectedSet := make(map[agent.AgentName]struct{}, len(selectedAgents))
   460→	for _, ag := range selectedAgents {
   461→		selectedSet[ag.Name()] = struct{}{}
   462→	}
   463→
   464→	var errs []error
   465→	for _, name := range installedNames {
   466→		if _, selected := selectedSet[name]; selected {
   467→			continue
   468→		}
   469→		ag, err := agent.Get(name)
   470→		if err != nil {
   471→			continue
   472→		}
   473→		hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
   474→		if !ok {
   475→			continue
   476→		}
   477→		if err := hookAgent.UninstallHooks(); err != nil {
   478→			errs = append(errs, fmt.Errorf("failed to uninstall %s hooks: %w", ag.Type(), err))
   479→		} else {
   480→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "Removed %s hooks\n", ag.Type())
   481→		}
   482→	}
   483→	return errors.Join(errs...)
   484→}
   485→
   486→// setupAgentHooks sets up hooks for a given agent.
   487→// Returns the number of hooks installed (0 if already installed).
   488→func setupAgentHooks(ag agent.Agent, localDev, forceHooks bool) (int, error) { //nolint:unparam // return value used by setupAgentHooksNonInteractive
   489→	hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
   490→	if !ok {
   491→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("agent %s does not support hooks", ag.Name())
   492→	}
   493→
   494→	count, err := hookAgent.InstallHooks(localDev, forceHooks)
   495→	if err != nil {
   496→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to install %s hooks: %w", ag.Name(), err)
   497→	}
   498→
   499→	return count, nil
   500→}
   501→
   502→// detectOrSelectAgent tries to auto-detect agents, or prompts the user to select.
   503→// Returns the detected/selected agents and any error.
   504→//
   505→// On first run (no hooks installed):
   506→//   - Single detected agent: used automatically
   507→//   - Multiple/no detected agents: interactive multi-select prompt
   508→//
   509→// On re-run (hooks already installed):
   510→//   - Always shows the interactive multi-select
   511→//   - Pre-selects only agents that have hooks installed (respects prior deselection)
   512→//
   513→// selectFn overrides the interactive prompt for testing. When nil, the real form is used.
   514→// It receives available agent names and returns the selected names.
   515→func detectOrSelectAgent(w io.Writer, selectFn func(available []string) ([]string, error)) ([]agent.Agent, error) {
   516→	// Check for agents with hooks already installed (re-run detection)
   517→	installedAgentNames := GetAgentsWithHooksInstalled()
   518→	hasInstalledHooks := len(installedAgentNames) > 0
   519→
   520→	// Try auto-detection
   521→	detected := agent.DetectAll()
   522→
   523→	// First run: use existing auto-detect shortcuts
   524→	if !hasInstalledHooks {
   525→		switch {
   526→		case len(detected) == 1:
   527→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "Detected agent: %s\n\n", detected[0].Type())
   528→			return detected, nil
   529→
   530→		case len(detected) > 1:
   531→			agentTypes := make([]string, 0, len(detected))
   532→			for _, ag := range detected {
   533→				agentTypes = append(agentTypes, string(ag.Type()))
   534→			}
   535→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "Detected multiple agents: %s\n", strings.Join(agentTypes, ", "))
   536→			fmt.Fprintln(w)
   537→		}
   538→	}
   539→
   540→	// Check if we can prompt interactively
   541→	if !canPromptInteractively() {
   542→		if hasInstalledHooks {
   543→			// Re-run without TTY — keep currently installed agents
   544→			agents := make([]agent.Agent, 0, len(installedAgentNames))
   545→			for _, name := range installedAgentNames {
   546→				ag, err := agent.Get(name)
   547→				if err != nil {
   548→					continue
   549→				}
   550→				agents = append(agents, ag)
   551→			}
   552→			return agents, nil
   553→		}
   554→		if len(detected) > 0 {
   555→			return detected, nil
   556→		}
   557→		defaultAgent := agent.Default()
   558→		if defaultAgent == nil {
   559→			return nil, errors.New("no default agent available")
   560→		}
   561→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "Agent: %s (use --agent to change)\n\n", defaultAgent.Type())
   562→		return []agent.Agent{defaultAgent}, nil
   563→	}
   564→
   565→	if !hasInstalledHooks && len(detected) == 0 {
   566→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "No agent configuration detected (e.g., .claude or .gemini directory).")
   567→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "This is normal - some agents don't require a config directory.")
   568→		fmt.Fprintln(w)
   569→	}
   570→
   571→	// Build pre-selection set.
   572→	// On re-run: only pre-select agents with hooks installed (respect prior deselection).
   573→	// On first run: pre-select all detected agents.
   574→	preSelectedSet := make(map[agent.AgentName]struct{})
   575→	if hasInstalledHooks {
   576→		for _, name := range installedAgentNames {
   577→			preSelectedSet[name] = struct{}{}
   578→		}
   579→	} else {
   580→		for _, ag := range detected {
   581→			preSelectedSet[ag.Name()] = struct{}{}
   582→		}
   583→	}
   584→
   585→	// Build options from registered agents
   586→	agentNames := agent.List()
   587→	options := make([]huh.Option[string], 0, len(agentNames))
   588→	for _, name := range agentNames {
   589→		ag, err := agent.Get(name)
   590→		if err != nil {
   591→			continue
   592→		}
   593→		// Only show agents that support hooks
   594→		if _, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport); !ok {
   595→			continue
   596→		}
   597→		opt := huh.NewOption(string(ag.Type()), string(name))
   598→		if _, isPreSelected := preSelectedSet[name]; isPreSelected {
   599→			opt = opt.Selected(true)
   600→		}
   601→		options = append(options, opt)
   602→	}
   603→
   604→	if len(options) == 0 {
   605→		return nil, errors.New("no agents with hook support available")
   606→	}
   607→
   608→	// Collect available agent names for the selector
   609→	availableNames := make([]string, 0, len(options))
   610→	for _, opt := range options {
   611→		availableNames = append(availableNames, opt.Value)
   612→	}
   613→
   614→	var selectedAgentNames []string
   615→	if selectFn != nil {
   616→		var err error
   617→		selectedAgentNames, err = selectFn(availableNames)
   618→		if err != nil {
   619→			return nil, err
   620→		}
   621→		if len(selectedAgentNames) == 0 {
   622→			return nil, errors.New("no agents selected")
   623→		}
   624→	} else {
   625→		form := NewAccessibleForm(
   626→			huh.NewGroup(
   627→				huh.NewMultiSelect[string]().
   628→					Title("Which agents are you using?").
   629→					Description("Use space to select, enter to confirm.").
   630→					Options(options...).
   631→					Validate(func(selected []string) error {
   632→						if len(selected) == 0 {
   633→							return errors.New("please select at least one agent")
   634→						}
   635→						return nil
   636→					}).
   637→					Value(&selectedAgentNames),
   638→			),
   639→		)
   640→		if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   641→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("agent selection cancelled: %w", err)
   642→		}
   643→	}
   644→
   645→	selectedAgents := make([]agent.Agent, 0, len(selectedAgentNames))
   646→	for _, name := range selectedAgentNames {
   647→		selectedAgent, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentName(name))
   648→		if err != nil {
   649→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get selected agent %s: %w", name, err)
   650→		}
   651→		selectedAgents = append(selectedAgents, selectedAgent)
   652→	}
   653→
   654→	agentTypes := make([]string, 0, len(selectedAgents))
   655→	for _, ag := range selectedAgents {
   656→		agentTypes = append(agentTypes, string(ag.Type()))
   657→	}
   658→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "\nSelected agents: %s\n\n", strings.Join(agentTypes, ", "))
   659→	return selectedAgents, nil
   660→}
   661→
   662→// canPromptInteractively checks if we can show interactive prompts.
   663→// Returns false when running in CI, tests, or other non-interactive environments.
   664→func canPromptInteractively() bool {
   665→	// Check for test environment
   666→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_TTY") != "" {
   667→		return os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_TTY") == "1"
   668→	}
   669→
   670→	// Check if /dev/tty is available
   671→	tty, err := os.OpenFile("/dev/tty", os.O_RDWR, 0)
   672→	if err != nil {
   673→		return false
   674→	}
   675→	_ = tty.Close()
   676→	return true
   677→}
   678→
   679→// printAgentError writes an error message followed by available agents and usage.
   680→func printAgentError(w io.Writer, message string) {
   681→	agents := agent.List()
   682→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "%s Available agents:\n", message)
   683→	fmt.Fprintln(w)
   684→	for _, a := range agents {
   685→		suffix := ""
   686→		if a == agent.DefaultAgentName {
   687→			suffix = "    (default)"
   688→		}
   689→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  %s%s\n", a, suffix)
   690→	}
   691→	fmt.Fprintln(w)
   692→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Usage: entire enable --agent <agent-name>")
   693→}
   694→
   695→// printMissingAgentError writes a helpful error listing available agents.
   696→func printMissingAgentError(w io.Writer) {
   697→	printAgentError(w, "Missing agent name.")
   698→}
   699→
   700→// printWrongAgentError writes a helpful error when an unknown agent name is provided.
   701→func printWrongAgentError(w io.Writer, name string) {
   702→	printAgentError(w, fmt.Sprintf("Unknown agent %q.", name))
   703→}
   704→
   705→// setupAgentHooksNonInteractive sets up hooks for a specific agent non-interactively.
   706→// If strategyName is provided, it sets the strategy; otherwise uses default.
   707→func setupAgentHooksNonInteractive(w io.Writer, ag agent.Agent, strategyName string, localDev, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry bool) error {
   708→	agentName := ag.Name()
   709→	// Check if agent supports hooks
   710→	hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
   711→	if !ok {
   712→		return fmt.Errorf("agent %s does not support hooks", agentName)
   713→	}
   714→
   715→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "Agent: %s\n\n", ag.Type())
   716→
   717→	// Install agent hooks (agent hooks don't depend on settings)
   718→	installedHooks, err := hookAgent.InstallHooks(localDev, forceHooks)
   719→	if err != nil {
   720→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install hooks for %s: %w", agentName, err)
   721→	}
   722→
   723→	// Setup .entire directory
   724→	if _, err := setupEntireDirectory(); err != nil {
   725→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup .entire directory: %w", err)
   726→	}
   727→
   728→	// Load existing settings to preserve other options (like strategy_options.push)
   729→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   730→	if err != nil {
   731→		// If we can't load, start with defaults
   732→		settings = &EntireSettings{Strategy: strategy.DefaultStrategyName}
   733→	}
   734→	settings.Enabled = true
   735→	if localDev {
   736→		settings.LocalDev = localDev
   737→	}
   738→
   739→	// Set push_sessions option if --skip-push-sessions flag was provided
   740→	if skipPushSessions {
   741→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   742→			settings.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]interface{})
   743→		}
   744→		settings.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"] = false
   745→	}
   746→
   747→	// Set strategy if provided
   748→	if strategyName != "" {
   749→		// Map display name to internal name if needed
   750→		internalStrategy := strategyName
   751→		if mapped, ok := strategyDisplayToInternal[strategyName]; ok {
   752→			internalStrategy = mapped
   753→		}
   754→		// Validate the strategy exists
   755→		if _, err := strategy.Get(internalStrategy); err != nil {
   756→			return fmt.Errorf("unknown strategy: %s (use manual-commit or auto-commit)", strategyName)
   757→		}
   758→		settings.Strategy = internalStrategy
   759→	}
   760→
   761→	// Handle telemetry for non-interactive mode
   762→	// Note: if telemetry is nil (not configured), it defaults to disabled
   763→	if !telemetry || os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT") != "" {
   764→		f := false
   765→		settings.Telemetry = &f
   766→	}
   767→
   768→	if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   769→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   770→	}
   771→
   772→	// Install git hooks AFTER saving settings (InstallGitHook reads local_dev from settings)
   773→	if _, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(true); err != nil {
   774→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
   775→	}
   776→	strategy.CheckAndWarnHookManagers(w)
   777→
   778→	if installedHooks == 0 {
   779→		msg := fmt.Sprintf("Hooks for %s already installed", ag.Description())
   780→		if ag.IsPreview() {
   781→			msg += " (Preview)"
   782→		}
   783→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "%s\n", msg)
   784→	} else {
   785→		msg := fmt.Sprintf("Installed %d hooks for %s", installedHooks, ag.Description())
   786→		if ag.IsPreview() {
   787→			msg += " (Preview)"
   788→		}
   789→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "%s\n", msg)
   790→	}
   791→
   792→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Project configured (%s)\n", configDisplayProject)
   793→
   794→	// Let the strategy handle its own setup requirements (creates entire/checkpoints/v1 branch, etc.)
   795→	strat, err := strategy.Get(settings.Strategy)
   796→	if err != nil {
   797→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get strategy: %w", err)
   798→	}
   799→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   800→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup strategy: %w", err)
   801→	}
   802→
   803→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nReady.")
   804→
   805→	return nil
   806→}

   808→// validateSetupFlags checks that --local and --project flags are not both specified.
   809→func validateSetupFlags(useLocal, useProject bool) error {
   810→	if useLocal && useProject {
   811→		return errors.New("cannot specify both --project and --local")
   812→	}
   813→	return nil
   814→}
   815→
   816→// determineSettingsTarget decides whether to write to settings.local.json based on:
   817→// - Whether settings.json already exists
   818→// - The --local and --project flags
   819→// Returns (useLocal, showNotification).
   820→func determineSettingsTarget(entireDir string, useLocal, useProject bool) (bool, bool) {
   821→	// Explicit --local flag always uses local settings
   822→	if useLocal {
   823→		return true, false
   824→	}
   825→
   826→	// Explicit --project flag always uses project settings
   827→	if useProject {
   828→		return false, false
   829→	}
   830→
   831→	// No flags specified - check if settings file exists
   832→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(entireDir, paths.SettingsFileName)
   833→	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsPath); err == nil {
   834→		// Settings file exists - auto-redirect to local with notification
   835→		return true, true
   836→	}
   837→
   838→	// Settings file doesn't exist - create it
   839→	return false, false
   840→}

   842→// setupEntireDirectory creates the .entire directory and gitignore.
   843→// Returns true if the directory was created, false if it already existed.
   844→func setupEntireDirectory() (bool, error) { //nolint:unparam // already present in codebase
   845→	// Get absolute path for the .entire directory
   846→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
   847→	if err != nil {
   848→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir // Fallback to relative
   849→	}
   850→
   851→	// Check if directory already exists
   852→	created := false
   853→	if _, err := os.Stat(entireDirAbs); os.IsNotExist(err) {
   854→		created = true
   855→	}
   856→
   857→	// Create .entire directory
   858→	//nolint:gosec // G301: Project directory needs standard permissions for git
   859→	if err := os.MkdirAll(entireDirAbs, 0o755); err != nil {
   860→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to create .entire directory: %w", err)
   861→	}
   862→
   863→	// Create/update .gitignore with all required entries
   864→	if err := strategy.EnsureEntireGitignore(); err != nil {
   865→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to setup .gitignore: %w", err)
   866→	}
   867→
   868→	return created, nil
   869→}

   897→func newCurlBashPostInstallCmd() *cobra.Command {
   898→	return &cobra.Command{
   899→		Use:    "curl-bash-post-install",
   900→		Short:  "Post-install tasks for curl|bash installer",
   901→		Hidden: true,
   902→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   903→			w := cmd.OutOrStdout()
   904→			if err := promptShellCompletion(w); err != nil {
   905→				fmt.Fprintf(w, "Note: Shell completion setup skipped: %v\n", err)

   907→			return nil

   911→
   912→// shellCompletionComment is the comment preceding the completion line
   913→const shellCompletionComment = "# Entire CLI shell completion"
   914→
   915→// errUnsupportedShell is returned when the user's shell is not supported for completion.
   916→var errUnsupportedShell = errors.New("unsupported shell")
   917→
   918→// shellCompletionTarget returns the rc file path and completion lines for the
   919→// user's current shell.
   920→func shellCompletionTarget() (shellName, rcFile, completionLine string, err error) {
   921→	home, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   922→	if err != nil {
   923→		return "", "", "", fmt.Errorf("cannot determine home directory: %w", err)
   924→	}
   925→
   926→	shell := os.Getenv("SHELL")
   927→	switch {
   928→	case strings.Contains(shell, "zsh"):
   929→		return "Zsh",
   930→			filepath.Join(home, ".zshrc"),
   931→			"autoload -Uz compinit && compinit && source <(entire completion zsh)",
   932→			nil
   933→	case strings.Contains(shell, "bash"):
   934→		bashRC := filepath.Join(home, ".bashrc")
   935→		if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(home, ".bash_profile")); err == nil {
   936→			bashRC = filepath.Join(home, ".bash_profile")
   937→		}
   938→		return "Bash",
   939→			bashRC,
   940→			"source <(entire completion bash)",
   941→			nil
   942→	case strings.Contains(shell, "fish"):
   943→		return "Fish",
   944→			filepath.Join(home, ".config", "fish", "config.fish"),
   945→			"entire completion fish | source",
   946→			nil
   947→	default:
   948→		return "", "", "", errUnsupportedShell
   949→	}
   950→}
   951→
   952→// promptShellCompletion offers to add shell completion to the user's rc file.
   953→// Only prompts if completion is not already configured.
   954→func promptShellCompletion(w io.Writer) error {
   955→	shellName, rcFile, completionLine, err := shellCompletionTarget()
   956→	if err != nil {
   957→		if errors.Is(err, errUnsupportedShell) {
   958→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "Note: Shell completion not available for your shell. Supported: zsh, bash, fish.\n")
   959→			return nil
   960→		}
   961→		return fmt.Errorf("shell completion: %w", err)
   962→	}
   963→
   964→	if isCompletionConfigured(rcFile) {
   965→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Shell completion already configured in %s\n", rcFile)
   966→		return nil
   967→	}
   968→
   969→	var selected string
   970→	form := NewAccessibleForm(
   971→		huh.NewGroup(
   972→			huh.NewSelect[string]().
   973→				Title(fmt.Sprintf("Enable shell completion? (detected: %s)", shellName)).
   974→				Options(
   975→					huh.NewOption("Yes", "yes"),
   976→					huh.NewOption("No", "no"),
   977→				).
   978→				Value(&selected),
   979→		),
   980→	)
   981→
   982→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   983→		//nolint:nilerr // User cancelled - not a fatal error, just skip
   984→		return nil
   985→	}
   986→
   987→	if selected != "yes" {
   988→		return nil
   989→	}
   990→
   991→	if err := appendShellCompletion(rcFile, completionLine); err != nil {
   992→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to update %s: %w", rcFile, err)
   993→	}
   994→
   995→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Shell completion added to %s\n", rcFile)
   996→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Restart your shell to activate")
   997→
   998→	return nil
   999→}
  1000→
  1001→// isCompletionConfigured checks if shell completion is already in the rc file.
  1002→func isCompletionConfigured(rcFile string) bool {
  1003→	//nolint:gosec // G304: rcFile is constructed from home dir + known filename, not user input
  1004→	content, err := os.ReadFile(rcFile)
  1005→	if err != nil {
  1006→		return false // File doesn't exist or can't read, treat as not configured
  1007→	}
  1008→	return strings.Contains(string(content), "entire completion")
  1009→}
  1010→
  1011→// appendShellCompletion adds the completion line to the rc file.
  1012→func appendShellCompletion(rcFile, completionLine string) error {
  1013→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(rcFile), 0o700); err != nil {
  1014→		return fmt.Errorf("creating directory: %w", err)
  1015→	}
  1016→	//nolint:gosec // G302: Shell rc files need 0644 for user readability
  1017→	f, err := os.OpenFile(rcFile, os.O_APPEND|os.O_CREATE|os.O_WRONLY, 0o644)
  1018→	if err != nil {
  1019→		return fmt.Errorf("opening file: %w", err)
  1020→	}
  1021→	defer f.Close()
  1022→
  1023→	_, err = f.WriteString("\n" + shellCompletionComment + "\n" + completionLine + "\n")
  1024→	if err != nil {
  1025→		return fmt.Errorf("writing completion: %w", err)
  1026→	}
  1027→	return nil
  1028→}
  1029→
  1030→// promptTelemetryConsent asks the user if they want to enable telemetry.
  1031→// It modifies settings.Telemetry based on the user's choice or flags.
  1032→// The caller is responsible for saving settings.
  1033→func promptTelemetryConsent(settings *EntireSettings, telemetryFlag bool) error {
  1034→	// Handle --telemetry=false flag first (always overrides existing setting)
  1035→	if !telemetryFlag {
  1036→		f := false
  1037→		settings.Telemetry = &f
  1038→		return nil
  1039→	}
  1040→
  1041→	// Skip if already asked
  1042→	if settings.Telemetry != nil {
  1043→		return nil
  1044→	}
  1045→
  1046→	// Skip if env var disables telemetry (record as disabled)
  1047→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT") != "" {
  1048→		f := false
  1049→		settings.Telemetry = &f
  1050→		return nil
  1051→	}
  1052→
  1053→	consent := true // Default to Yes
  1054→	form := NewAccessibleForm(
  1055→		huh.NewGroup(
  1056→			huh.NewConfirm().
  1057→				Title("Help improve Entire CLI?").
  1058→				Description("Share anonymous usage data. No code or personal info collected.").
  1059→				Affirmative("Yes").
  1060→				Negative("No").
  1061→				Value(&consent),
  1062→		),
  1063→	)
  1064→
  1065→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
  1066→		return fmt.Errorf("telemetry prompt: %w", err)
  1067→	}
  1068→
  1069→	settings.Telemetry = &consent
  1070→	return nil
  1071→}
