```
  1073→// runUninstall completely removes Entire from the repository.
  1074→func runUninstall(w, errW io.Writer, force bool) error {
  1075→	// Check if we're in a git repository
  1076→	if _, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err != nil {
  1077→		fmt.Fprintln(errW, "Not a git repository. Nothing to uninstall.")
  1078→		return NewSilentError(errors.New("not a git repository"))
  1079→	}
  1080→
  1081→	// Gather counts for display
  1082→	sessionStateCount := countSessionStates()
  1083→	shadowBranchCount := countShadowBranches()
  1084→	gitHooksInstalled := strategy.IsGitHookInstalled()
  1085→	agentsWithInstalledHooks := GetAgentsWithHooksInstalled()
  1086→	entireDirExists := checkEntireDirExists()
  1087→
  1088→	// Check if there's anything to uninstall
  1089→	if !entireDirExists && !gitHooksInstalled && sessionStateCount == 0 &&
  1090→		shadowBranchCount == 0 && len(agentsWithInstalledHooks) == 0 {
  1091→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "Entire is not installed in this repository.")
  1092→		return nil
  1093→	}
  1094→
  1095→	// Show confirmation prompt unless --force
  1096→	if !force {
  1097→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nThis will completely remove Entire from this repository:")
  1098→		if entireDirExists {
  1099→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - .entire/ directory")
  1100→		}
  1101→		if gitHooksInstalled {
  1102→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Git hooks (prepare-commit-msg, commit-msg, post-commit, pre-push)")
  1103→		}
  1104→		if sessionStateCount > 0 {
  1105→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "  - Session state files (%d)\n", sessionStateCount)
  1106→		}
  1107→		if shadowBranchCount > 0 {
  1108→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "  - Shadow branches (%d)\n", shadowBranchCount)
  1109→		}
  1110→		if len(agentsWithInstalledHooks) > 0 {
  1111→			displayNames := make([]string, 0, len(agentsWithInstalledHooks))
  1112→			for _, name := range agentsWithInstalledHooks {
  1113→				if ag, err := agent.Get(name); err == nil {
  1114→					displayNames = append(displayNames, string(ag.Type()))
  1115→				}
  1116→			}
  1117→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "  - Agent hooks (%s)\n", strings.Join(displayNames, ", "))
  1118→		}
  1119→		fmt.Fprintln(w)
  1120→
  1121→		var confirmed bool
  1122→		form := NewAccessibleForm(
  1123→			huh.NewGroup(
  1124→				huh.NewConfirm().
  1125→					Title("Are you sure you want to uninstall Entire?").
  1126→					Affirmative("Yes, uninstall").
  1127→					Negative("Cancel").
  1128→					Value(&confirmed),
  1129→			),
  1130→		)
  1131→
  1132→		if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
  1133→			return fmt.Errorf("confirmation cancelled: %w", err)
  1134→		}
  1135→
  1136→		if !confirmed {
  1137→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "Uninstall cancelled.")
  1138→			return nil
  1139→		}
  1140→	}
  1141→
  1142→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nUninstalling Entire CLI...")
  1143→
  1144→	// 1. Remove agent hooks (lowest risk)
  1145→	if err := removeAgentHooks(w); err != nil {
  1146→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove agent hooks: %v\n", err)
  1147→	}
  1148→
  1149→	// 2. Remove git hooks
  1150→	removed, err := strategy.RemoveGitHook()
  1151→	if err != nil {
  1152→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove git hooks: %v\n", err)
  1153→	} else if removed > 0 {
  1154→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Removed git hooks (%d)\n", removed)
  1155→	}
  1156→
  1157→	// 3. Remove session state files
  1158→	statesRemoved, err := removeAllSessionStates()
  1159→	if err != nil {
  1160→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove session states: %v\n", err)
  1161→	} else if statesRemoved > 0 {
  1162→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Removed session states (%d)\n", statesRemoved)
  1163→	}
  1164→
  1165→	// 4. Remove .entire/ directory
  1166→	if err := removeEntireDirectory(); err != nil {
  1167→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove .entire directory: %v\n", err)
  1168→	} else if entireDirExists {
  1169→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Removed .entire directory")
  1170→	}
  1171→
  1172→	// 5. Remove shadow branches
  1173→	branchesRemoved, err := removeAllShadowBranches()
  1174→	if err != nil {
  1175→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove shadow branches: %v\n", err)
  1176→	} else if branchesRemoved > 0 {
  1177→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Removed %d shadow branches\n", branchesRemoved)
  1178→	}
  1179→
  1180→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nEntire CLI uninstalled successfully.")
  1181→	return nil
  1182→}
  1183→
  1184→// countSessionStates returns the number of active session state files.
  1185→func countSessionStates() int {
  1186→	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
  1187→	if err != nil {
  1188→		return 0
  1189→	}
  1190→	states, err := store.List(context.Background())
  1191→	if err != nil {
  1192→		return 0
  1193→	}
  1194→	return len(states)
  1195→}
  1196→
  1197→// countShadowBranches returns the number of shadow branches.
  1198→func countShadowBranches() int {
  1199→	branches, err := strategy.ListShadowBranches()
  1200→	if err != nil {
  1201→		return 0
  1202→	}
  1203→	return len(branches)
  1204→}
  1205→
  1206→// checkEntireDirExists checks if the .entire directory exists.
  1207→func checkEntireDirExists() bool {
  1208→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
  1209→	if err != nil {
  1210→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir
  1211→	}
  1212→	_, err = os.Stat(entireDirAbs)
  1213→	return err == nil
  1214→}
  1215→
  1216→// removeAgentHooks removes hooks from all agents that support hooks.
  1217→func removeAgentHooks(w io.Writer) error {
  1218→	var errs []error
  1219→	for _, name := range agent.List() {
  1220→		ag, err := agent.Get(name)
  1221→		if err != nil {
  1222→			continue
  1223→		}
  1224→		hs, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
  1225→		if !ok {
  1226→			continue
  1227→		}
  1228→		wasInstalled := hs.AreHooksInstalled()
  1229→		if err := hs.UninstallHooks(); err != nil {
  1230→			errs = append(errs, err)
  1231→		} else if wasInstalled {
  1232→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Removed %s hooks\n", ag.Type())
  1233→		}
  1234→	}
  1235→	return errors.Join(errs...)
  1236→}
  1237→
  1238→// removeAllSessionStates removes all session state files and the directory.
  1239→func removeAllSessionStates() (int, error) {
  1240→	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
  1241→	if err != nil {
  1242→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create state store: %w", err)
  1243→	}
  1244→
  1245→	// Count states before removing
  1246→	states, err := store.List(context.Background())
  1247→	if err != nil {
  1248→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
  1249→	}
  1250→	count := len(states)
  1251→
  1252→	// Remove the entire directory
  1253→	if err := store.RemoveAll(); err != nil {
  1254→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to remove session states: %w", err)
  1255→	}
  1256→
  1257→	return count, nil
  1258→}
  1259→
  1260→// removeEntireDirectory removes the .entire directory.
  1261→func removeEntireDirectory() error {
  1262→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
  1263→	if err != nil {
  1264→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir
  1265→	}
  1266→	if err := os.RemoveAll(entireDirAbs); err != nil {
  1267→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove .entire directory: %w", err)
  1268→	}
  1269→	return nil
  1270→}
  1271→
  1272→// removeAllShadowBranches removes all shadow branches.
  1273→func removeAllShadowBranches() (int, error) {
  1274→	branches, err := strategy.ListShadowBranches()
  1275→	if err != nil {
  1276→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to list shadow branches: %w", err)
  1277→	}
  1278→	if len(branches) == 0 {
  1279→		return 0, nil
  1280→	}
  1281→	deleted, _, err := strategy.DeleteShadowBranches(branches)
  1282→	return len(deleted), err
  1283→}
```

```
    10→
    11→// ManualCommitStrategy implements the manual-commit strategy for session management.
    12→// It stores checkpoints on shadow branches and condenses session logs to a
    13→// permanent sessions branch when the user commits.
    14→type ManualCommitStrategy struct {
    15→	// stateStore manages session state files in .git/entire-sessions/
    16→	stateStore *session.StateStore
    17→	// stateStoreOnce ensures thread-safe lazy initialization
    18→	stateStoreOnce sync.Once
    19→	// stateStoreErr captures any error during initialization
    20→	stateStoreErr error
    21→
    22→	// checkpointStore manages checkpoint data in git
    23→	checkpointStore *checkpoint.GitStore
    24→	// checkpointStoreOnce ensures thread-safe lazy initialization
    25→	checkpointStoreOnce sync.Once
    26→	// checkpointStoreErr captures any error during initialization
    27→	checkpointStoreErr error
    28→}
    29→
    30→// getStateStore returns the session state store, initializing it lazily if needed.
    31→// Thread-safe via sync.Once.
    32→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getStateStore() (*session.StateStore, error) {
    33→	s.stateStoreOnce.Do(func() {
    34→		store, err := session.NewStateStore()
    35→		if err != nil {
    36→			s.stateStoreErr = fmt.Errorf("failed to create state store: %w", err)
    37→			return
    38→		}
    39→		s.stateStore = store
    40→	})
    41→	return s.stateStore, s.stateStoreErr
    42→}
    43→
    44→// getCheckpointStore returns the checkpoint store, initializing it lazily if needed.
    45→// Thread-safe via sync.Once.
    46→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getCheckpointStore() (*checkpoint.GitStore, error) {
    47→	s.checkpointStoreOnce.Do(func() {
    48→		repo, err := OpenRepository()
    49→		if err != nil {
    50→			s.checkpointStoreErr = fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
    51→			return
    52→		}
    53→		s.checkpointStore = checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)
    54→	})
    55→	return s.checkpointStore, s.checkpointStoreErr
    56→}
    57→
    58→// NewManualCommitStrategy creates a new manual-commit strategy instance.
    59→//
    60→
    61→func NewManualCommitStrategy() Strategy {
    62→	return &ManualCommitStrategy{}
    63→}
    64→
    65→// NewShadowStrategy creates a new manual-commit strategy instance.
    66→// This legacy constructor delegates to NewManualCommitStrategy.
    67→//
    68→
    69→func NewShadowStrategy() Strategy {
    70→	return NewManualCommitStrategy()
    71→}
    72→
    73→// Name returns the strategy name.
    74→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) Name() string {
    75→	return StrategyNameManualCommit
    76→}
    77→
    78→// Description returns the strategy description.
    79→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) Description() string {
    80→	return "Manual commit checkpoints with session logs on entire/checkpoints/v1"
    81→}
    82→
    83→// ValidateRepository validates that the repository is suitable for this strategy.
    84→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) ValidateRepository() error {
    85→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
    86→	if err != nil {
    87→		return fmt.Errorf("not a git repository: %w", err)
    88→	}
    89→
    90→	_, err = repo.Worktree()
    91→	if err != nil {
    92→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to access worktree: %w", err)
    93→	}
    94→
    95→	return nil
    96→}
    97→
    98→// EnsureSetup ensures the strategy is properly set up.
    99→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) EnsureSetup() error {
   100→	if err := EnsureEntireGitignore(); err != nil {
   101→		return err
   102→	}
   103→
   104→	// Ensure the entire/checkpoints/v1 orphan branch exists for permanent session storage
   105→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   106→	if err != nil {
   107→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   108→	}
   109→	if err := EnsureMetadataBranch(repo); err != nil {
   110→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to ensure metadata branch: %w", err)
   111→	}
   112→
   113→	// Install generic hooks (they delegate to strategy at runtime)
   114→	if !IsGitHookInstalled() {
   115→		if _, err := InstallGitHook(true); err != nil {
   116→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
   117→		}
   118→	}
   119→	return nil
   120→}

   125→//   - Session state files with no corresponding checkpoints or shadow branches
   126→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) ListOrphanedItems() ([]CleanupItem, error) {
   127→	var items []CleanupItem
   128→
   129→	// Shadow branches (should have been auto-cleaned after condensation)
   130→	branches, err := ListShadowBranches()
```

```
   450→// For auto-commit strategy, this returns nil since git reset doesn't delete untracked files.
   451→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) PreviewRewind(_ RewindPoint) (*RewindPreview, error) {
   452→	// Auto-commit uses git reset --hard which doesn't affect untracked files
   453→	// Return empty preview to indicate no untracked files will be deleted
   454→	return &RewindPreview{}, nil
   455→}
   456→
   457→// EnsureSetup ensures the strategy's required setup is in place.
   458→// For auto-commit strategy:
   459→// - Ensure .entire/.gitignore has all required entries
   460→// - Create orphan entire/checkpoints/v1 branch if it doesn't exist
   461→// - Install git hooks if missing (self-healing for third-party overwrites)
   462→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) EnsureSetup() error {
   463→	if err := EnsureEntireGitignore(); err != nil {
   464→		return err
   465→	}
   466→
   467→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   468→	if err != nil {
   469→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   470→	}
   471→
   472→	// Ensure the entire/checkpoints/v1 orphan branch exists
   473→	if err := EnsureMetadataBranch(repo); err != nil {
   474→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to ensure metadata branch: %w", err)
   475→	}
   476→
   477→	// Install generic hooks if missing (they delegate to strategy at runtime)
   478→	if !IsGitHookInstalled() {
   479→		if _, err := InstallGitHook(true); err != nil {
   480→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
   481→		}
   482→	}
   483→
   484→	return nil
   485→}
   486→
   487→// GetSessionInfo returns session information for linking commits.
   488→// For auto-commit strategy, we don't track active sessions - metadata is stored on
   489→// entire/checkpoints/v1 branch when SaveStep is called. Active branch commits
   490→// are kept clean (no trailers), so this returns ErrNoSession.
   491→// Use ListSessions() or GetSession() to retrieve session info from the metadata branch.
   492→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) GetSessionInfo() (*SessionInfo, error) {
   493→	// Dual strategy doesn't track active sessions like shadow does.
   494→	// Session metadata is stored on entire/checkpoints/v1 branch and can be
   495→	// retrieved via ListSessions() or GetSession().
   496→	return nil, ErrNoSession
   497→}
```

```
    29→// SessionIDConflictError is returned when trying to start a new session
    30→// but the shadow branch already has commits from a different session ID.
    31→// This prevents orphaning existing session work.
    32→type SessionIDConflictError struct {
    33→	ExistingSession string // Session ID found in the shadow branch
    34→	NewSession      string // Session ID being initialized
    35→	ShadowBranch    string // The shadow branch name (e.g., "entire/abc1234")

    42→// ExtractToolUseIDFromTaskMetadataDir extracts the ToolUseID from a task metadata directory path.
    43→// Task metadata dirs have format: .entire/metadata/<session>/tasks/<toolUseID>
    44→// Returns empty string if not a task metadata directory.
    45→func ExtractToolUseIDFromTaskMetadataDir(metadataDir string) string {
    46→	parts := strings.Split(metadataDir, "/")
    47→	if len(parts) >= 2 && parts[len(parts)-2] == "tasks" {
    48→		return parts[len(parts)-1]
    49→	}
    50→	return ""
    51→}

   291→// ReadTaskCheckpoint reads the checkpoint.json file from a task metadata directory.
   292→// This is used during rewind to get the checkpoint UUID for transcript truncation.
   293→func ReadTaskCheckpoint(taskMetadataDir string) (*TaskCheckpoint, error) {
   294→	checkpointFile := taskMetadataDir + "/checkpoint.json"
   295→
   296→	data, err := os.ReadFile(checkpointFile) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled git metadata path
   297→	if err != nil {
   298→		return nil, err //nolint:wrapcheck // already present in codebase
   299→	}
   300→
   301→	var checkpoint TaskCheckpoint
   302→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &checkpoint); err != nil {
   303→		return nil, err //nolint:wrapcheck // already present in codebase
   304→	}
   305→
   306→	return &checkpoint, nil
   307→}

   309→// Strategy defines the interface for git operation strategies.
   310→// Different implementations can use commits, branches, stashes, etc.
   311→//
   312→// Note: State capture (tracking untracked files before a session) is handled
   313→// by the CLI layer, not the strategy. The strategy receives pre-computed
   314→// file lists in StepContext.
   315→type Strategy interface {
   316→	// Name returns the strategy identifier (e.g., "commit", "branch", "stash")
   317→	Name() string
   318→
   319→	// Description returns a human-readable description for the setup wizard
   320→	Description() string
   321→
   322→	// ValidateRepository checks if the repository is in a valid state
   323→	// for this strategy to operate. Returns an error if validation fails.
   324→	ValidateRepository() error
   325→
   326→	// SaveStep is called on Stop to save all session changes
   327→	// using this strategy's approach (commit, branch, stash, etc.)
   328→	SaveStep(ctx StepContext) error
   329→
   330→	// SaveTaskStep is called by PostToolUse[Task] hook when a subagent completes.
   331→	// Creates a checkpoint commit with task metadata for later rewind.
   332→	// Different strategies may handle this differently:
   333→	// - Commit strategy: commits to active branch
   334→	// - Manual-commit strategy: commits to shadow branch
   335→	// - Auto-commit strategy: commits logs to shadow only (code deferred to Stop)
   336→	SaveTaskStep(ctx TaskStepContext) error
   337→
   338→	// GetRewindPoints returns available points to rewind to.
   339→	// The limit parameter controls the maximum number of points to return.
   340→	GetRewindPoints(limit int) ([]RewindPoint, error)
   341→
   342→	// Rewind restores the repository to the given rewind point.
   343→	// The metadataDir in the point is used to restore the session transcript.
   344→	Rewind(point RewindPoint) error
   345→
   346→	// CanRewind checks if rewinding is currently possible.
   347→	// Returns (canRewind, reason if not, error)
   348→	CanRewind() (bool, string, error)
   349→
   350→	// PreviewRewind returns what will happen if rewinding to the given point.
   351→	// This allows showing warnings about files that will be deleted before the rewind.
   352→	// Returns nil if preview is not supported (e.g., auto-commit strategy).
   353→	PreviewRewind(point RewindPoint) (*RewindPreview, error)
   354→
   355→	// GetTaskCheckpoint returns the task checkpoint for a given rewind point.
   356→	// For strategies that store checkpoints in git (auto-commit), this reads from the branch.
   357→	// For strategies that store checkpoints on disk (commit, manual-commit), this reads from the filesystem.
   358→	// Returns nil, nil if not a task checkpoint or checkpoint not found.
   359→	GetTaskCheckpoint(point RewindPoint) (*TaskCheckpoint, error)
   360→
   361→	// GetTaskCheckpointTranscript returns the session transcript for a task checkpoint.
   362→	// For strategies that store transcripts in git (auto-commit), this reads from the branch.
   363→	// For strategies that store transcripts on disk (commit, manual-commit), this reads from the filesystem.
   364→	GetTaskCheckpointTranscript(point RewindPoint) ([]byte, error)
   365→
   366→	// GetSessionInfo returns session information for linking commits.
   367→	// This is used by the context command to generate trailers.
   368→	// Returns ErrNoSession if no session info is available.
   369→	GetSessionInfo() (*SessionInfo, error)
   370→
   371→	// EnsureSetup ensures the strategy's required setup is in place,
   372→	// installing any missing pieces (git hooks, gitignore entries, etc.).
   373→	// Returns nil if setup is complete or was successfully installed.
   374→	EnsureSetup() error
   375→
   376→	// NOTE: ListSessions and GetSession are standalone functions in session.go.
   377→	// They read from entire/checkpoints/v1 and merge with SessionSource if implemented.
   378→
   379→	// GetMetadataRef returns a reference to the metadata commit for the given checkpoint.
   380→	// Format: "<branch>@<commit-sha>" (e.g., "entire/checkpoints/v1@abc123").
   381→	// Returns empty string if not applicable (e.g., commit strategy with filesystem metadata).
   382→	GetMetadataRef(checkpoint Checkpoint) string
   383→
   384→	// GetSessionMetadataRef returns a reference to the most recent metadata commit for a session.
   385→	// Format: "<branch>@<commit-sha>" (e.g., "entire/checkpoints/v1@abc123").
   386→	// Returns empty string if not applicable or session not found.
   387→	GetSessionMetadataRef(sessionID string) string
   388→
   389→	// GetSessionContext returns the context.md content for a session.
   390→	// Returns empty string if not available.
   391→	GetSessionContext(sessionID string) string
   392→
   393→	// GetCheckpointLog returns the session transcript for a specific checkpoint.
   394→	// For strategies that store transcripts in git branches (auto-commit, manual-commit),
   395→	// this reads from the checkpoint's commit tree.
   396→	// For strategies that store on disk (commit), reads from the filesystem.
   397→	// Returns ErrNoMetadata if transcript is not available.
   398→	GetCheckpointLog(checkpoint Checkpoint) ([]byte, error)
   399→}
   400→
   401→// SessionInitializer is an optional interface for strategies that need to
   402→// initialize session state when a user prompt is submitted.
   403→// Strategies like manual-commit use this to create session state files that
   404→// the git prepare-commit-msg hook can detect.
   405→type SessionInitializer interface {
   406→	// InitializeSession creates session state for a new session.
   407→	// Called during UserPromptSubmit hook before any checkpoints are created.
   408→	// agentType is the human-readable name of the agent (e.g., "Claude Code").
   409→	// transcriptPath is the path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection).
   410→	// userPrompt is the user's prompt text (stored truncated as FirstPrompt for display).
   411→	InitializeSession(sessionID string, agentType agent.AgentType, transcriptPath string, userPrompt string) error
   412→}
   413→
   414→// PrepareCommitMsgHandler is an optional interface for strategies that need to
   415→// handle the git prepare-commit-msg hook.
   416→type PrepareCommitMsgHandler interface {
   417→	// PrepareCommitMsg is called by the git prepare-commit-msg hook.
   418→	// It can modify the commit message file to add trailers, etc.
   419→	// The source parameter indicates how the commit was initiated:
   420→	//   - "" or "template": normal editor flow
   421→	//   - "message": using -m or -F flag
   422→	//   - "merge": merge commit
   423→	//   - "squash": squash merge
   424→	//   - "commit": amend with -c/-C
   425→	// Should return nil on errors to not block commits (log warnings to stderr).
   426→	PrepareCommitMsg(commitMsgFile string, source string) error
   427→}
   428→
   429→// PostCommitHandler is an optional interface for strategies that need to
   430→// handle the git post-commit hook.
   431→type PostCommitHandler interface {
   432→	// PostCommit is called by the git post-commit hook after a commit is created.
   433→	// Used to perform actions like condensing session data after commits.
   434→	// Should return nil on errors to not block subsequent operations (log warnings to stderr).
   435→	PostCommit() error
   436→}
   437→
   438→// CommitMsgHandler is an optional interface for strategies that need to
   439→// handle the git commit-msg hook.
   440→type CommitMsgHandler interface {
   441→	// CommitMsg is called by the git commit-msg hook after the user edits the message.
   442→	// Used to validate or modify the final commit message before the commit is created.
   443→	// If this returns an error, the commit is aborted.
   444→	CommitMsg(commitMsgFile string) error
   445→}
   446→
   447→// PrePushHandler is an optional interface for strategies that need to
   448→// handle the git pre-push hook.
   449→type PrePushHandler interface {
   450→	// PrePush is called by the git pre-push hook before pushing to a remote.
   451→	// Used to push session branches (e.g., entire/checkpoints/v1) alongside user pushes.
   452→	// The remote parameter is the name of the remote being pushed to.
   453→	// Should return nil on errors to not block pushes (log warnings to stderr).
   454→	PrePush(remote string) error
   455→}
   456→
   457→// TurnEndHandler is an optional interface for strategies that need to
   458→// perform work when an agent turn ends (ACTIVE → IDLE).
   459→// For example, manual-commit strategy uses this to finalize checkpoints
   460→// with the full session transcript.
   461→type TurnEndHandler interface {
   462→	// HandleTurnEnd performs strategy-specific cleanup at the end of a turn.
   463→	// Work items are read from state (e.g. TurnCheckpointIDs), not from the
   464→	// action list. The state has already been updated by ApplyTransition;
   465→	// the caller saves it after this method returns.
   466→	HandleTurnEnd(state *session.State) error
   467→}
   468→
   469→// RestoredSession describes a single session that was restored by RestoreLogsOnly.
   470→// Each session may come from a different agent, so callers use this to print
   471→// per-session resume commands without re-reading the metadata tree.
   472→type RestoredSession struct {
   473→	SessionID string
   474→	Agent     agent.AgentType
   475→	Prompt    string
   476→}
   477→
   478→// LogsOnlyRestorer is an optional interface for strategies that support
   479→// restoring session logs without file state restoration.
   480→// This is used for "logs-only" rewind points where only the session transcript
   481→// can be restored (file state requires git checkout).
   482→type LogsOnlyRestorer interface {
   483→	// RestoreLogsOnly restores session logs from a logs-only rewind point.
   484→	// Does not modify the working directory - only restores the transcript
   485→	// to the agent's session directory (determined per-session from checkpoint metadata).
   486→	// If force is false, prompts for confirmation when local logs have newer timestamps.
   487→	// Returns info about each restored session so callers can print correct resume commands.
   488→	RestoreLogsOnly(point RewindPoint, force bool) ([]RestoredSession, error)
   489→}
   490→
   491→// SessionResetter is an optional interface for strategies that support
   492→// resetting session state and shadow branches.
   493→// This is used by the "reset" command to clean up shadow branches
   494→// and session state when a user wants to start fresh.
   495→type SessionResetter interface {
   496→	// Reset deletes the shadow branch and session state for the current HEAD.
   497→	// Returns nil if there's nothing to reset (no shadow branch).
   498→	Reset() error
   499→
   500→	// ResetSession clears the state for a single session and cleans up
   501→	// the shadow branch if no other sessions reference it.
   502→	// File changes remain in the working directory.
   503→	ResetSession(sessionID string) error
   504→}
   505→
   506→// SessionCondenser is an optional interface for strategies that support
   507→// force-condensing a session. This is used by "entire doctor" to
   508→// salvage stuck sessions by condensing their data to permanent storage.
   509→type SessionCondenser interface {
   510→	// CondenseSessionByID force-condenses a session and cleans up.
   511→	// Generates a new checkpoint ID, condenses to entire/checkpoints/v1,
   512→	// updates the session state, and removes the shadow branch
   513→	// if no other active sessions need it.
   514→	CondenseSessionByID(sessionID string) error
   515→}
   516→
   517→// ConcurrentSessionChecker is an optional interface for strategies that support
   518→// counting concurrent sessions with uncommitted changes.
   519→// This is used by the SessionStart hook to show an informational message about
   520→// how many other active conversations will be included in the next commit.
   521→type ConcurrentSessionChecker interface {
   522→	// CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints returns the number of other active sessions
   523→	// with uncommitted checkpoints on the same base commit.
   524→	// Returns 0, nil if no such sessions exist.
   525→	CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints(currentSessionID string) (int, error)
   526→}
   527→
   528→// SessionSource is an optional interface for strategies that provide additional
   529→// sessions beyond those stored on the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   530→// For example, manual-commit strategy provides active sessions from .git/entire-sessions/
   531→// that haven't yet been condensed to entire/checkpoints/v1.
   532→//
   533→// ListSessions() automatically discovers all registered strategies, checks if they
   534→// implement SessionSource, and merges their additional sessions by ID.
   535→type SessionSource interface {
   536→	// GetAdditionalSessions returns sessions not yet on entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   537→	GetAdditionalSessions() ([]*Session, error)
   538→}
   539→
   540→// OrphanedItemsLister is an optional interface for strategies that can identify
   541→// orphaned items (shadow branches, session states, checkpoints) that should be
   542→// cleaned up. This is used by the "entire session cleanup" command.
   543→//
   544→// ListAllCleanupItems() automatically discovers all registered strategies, checks
   545→// if they implement OrphanedItemsLister, and combines their orphaned items.
   546→type OrphanedItemsLister interface {
   547→	// ListOrphanedItems returns items created by this strategy that are now orphaned.
   548→	// Each strategy defines what "orphaned" means for its own data structures.
   549→	ListOrphanedItems() ([]CleanupItem, error)
   550→}
   551→
```

```
   155→trap 'git checkout "$current_branch" --quiet 2>/dev/null; [ "$has_changes" = true ] && git stash pop --quiet 2>/dev/null; rm -rf "$tmpdir"' EXIT
   156→
   157→echo "=== Benchmarking current branch ($current_branch) ==="
   158→go test -bench="$BENCH_PATTERN" -benchmem -run='^$' -count="$BENCH_COUNT" -timeout="$BENCH_TIMEOUT" ./... > "$new_out" 2>&1 || true

   168→echo ""

   178→fi

   180→echo ""
```

```
     1→// Package benchutil provides test fixture helpers for CLI benchmarks.
     2→//
     3→// It creates realistic git repositories, transcripts, session states,
     4→// and checkpoint data for benchmarking the hot paths (SaveStep, PostCommit/Condense).
     5→package benchutil
     6→
     7→import (
     8→	"context"
     9→	"encoding/json"
    10→	"fmt"
    11→	"os"
    12→	"os/exec"
    13→	"path/filepath"
    14→	"strings"
    15→	"testing"
    16→	"time"
    17→
    18→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    19→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint"
    20→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint/id"
    21→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/jsonutil"
    22→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    23→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session"
    24→
    25→	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5"
    26→	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing"
    27→	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/object"
    28→)
    29→
    30→// BenchRepo is a fully initialized git repository with Entire configured,
    31→// ready for checkpoint benchmarks.
    32→type BenchRepo struct {
    33→	// Dir is the absolute path to the repository root.
    34→	Dir string
    35→
    36→	// Repo is the go-git repository handle.
    37→	Repo *git.Repository
    38→
    39→	// Store is the checkpoint GitStore for this repo.
    40→	Store *checkpoint.GitStore
    41→
    42→	// HeadHash is the current HEAD commit hash string.
    43→	HeadHash string
    44→
    45→	// WorktreeID is the worktree identifier (empty for main worktree).
    46→	WorktreeID string
    47→}
    48→
    49→// RepoOpts configures how NewBenchRepo creates the test repository.
    50→type RepoOpts struct {
    51→	// FileCount is the number of tracked files to create in the initial commit.
    52→	// Each file is ~100 lines of Go code. Defaults to 10.
    53→	FileCount int
    54→
    55→	// FileSizeLines is the number of lines per file. Defaults to 100.
    56→	FileSizeLines int
    57→
    58→	// CommitCount is the number of commits to create. Defaults to 1.
    59→	CommitCount int
    60→
    61→	// Strategy is the strategy name for .entire/settings.json.
    62→	// Defaults to "manual-commit".
    63→	Strategy string
    64→
    65→	// FeatureBranch, if non-empty, creates and checks out this branch
    66→	// after the initial commits.
    67→	FeatureBranch string
    68→}
    69→
    70→func (o *RepoOpts) withDefaults() RepoOpts {
    71→	out := *o
    72→	if out.FileCount == 0 {
    73→		out.FileCount = 10
    74→	}
    75→	if out.FileSizeLines == 0 {
    76→		out.FileSizeLines = 100
    77→	}
    78→	if out.CommitCount == 0 {
    79→		out.CommitCount = 1
    80→	}
    81→	if out.Strategy == "" {
    82→		out.Strategy = "manual-commit"
    83→	}
    84→	return out
    85→}
    86→
    87→// NewBenchRepo creates an isolated git repository for benchmarks.
    88→// The repo has an initial commit with the configured number of files,
    89→// a .gitignore excluding .entire/, and Entire settings initialized.
    90→//
    91→// Uses b.TempDir() so cleanup is automatic.
    92→func NewBenchRepo(b *testing.B, opts RepoOpts) *BenchRepo {
    93→	b.Helper()
    94→	opts = opts.withDefaults()
    95→
    96→	dir := b.TempDir()
    97→	// Resolve symlinks (macOS /var -> /private/var)
    98→	if resolved, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(dir); err == nil {
    99→		dir = resolved
   100→	}
   101→
   102→	// Init repo
   103→	repo, err := git.PlainInit(dir, false)
   104→	if err != nil {
   105→		b.Fatalf("git init: %v", err)
   106→	}
   107→
   108→	// Create .gitignore and .entire settings
   109→	writeFile(b, dir, ".gitignore", ".entire/\n")
   110→	initEntireSettings(b, dir, opts.Strategy)
   111→
   112→	// Generate initial files
   113→	wt, err := repo.Worktree()
   114→	if err != nil {
   115→		b.Fatalf("worktree: %v", err)
   116→	}
   117→
   118→	for i := range opts.FileCount {
   119→		name := fmt.Sprintf("src/file_%03d.go", i)
   120→		content := GenerateGoFile(i, opts.FileSizeLines)
   121→		writeFile(b, dir, name, content)
   122→		if _, err := wt.Add(name); err != nil {
   123→			b.Fatalf("add %s: %v", name, err)
   124→		}
   125→	}
   126→	if _, err := wt.Add(".gitignore"); err != nil {
   127→		b.Fatalf("add .gitignore: %v", err)
   128→	}
   129→
   130→	// Create commits
   131→	var headHash plumbing.Hash
   132→	for c := range opts.CommitCount {
   133→		if c > 0 {
   134→			// Modify a file for subsequent commits
   135→			name := fmt.Sprintf("src/file_%03d.go", c%opts.FileCount)
   136→			content := GenerateGoFile(c*1000, opts.FileSizeLines)
   137→			writeFile(b, dir, name, content)
   138→			if _, err := wt.Add(name); err != nil {
   139→				b.Fatalf("add %s: %v", name, err)
   140→			}
   141→		}
   142→		headHash, err = wt.Commit(fmt.Sprintf("Commit %d", c+1), &git.CommitOptions{
   143→			Author: &object.Signature{
   144→				Name:  "Bench User",
   145→				Email: "bench@example.com",
   146→				When:  time.Now(),
   147→			},
   148→		})
   149→		if err != nil {
   150→			b.Fatalf("commit %d: %v", c+1, err)
   151→		}
   152→	}
   153→
   154→	// Optionally create feature branch
   155→	if opts.FeatureBranch != "" {
   156→		ref := plumbing.NewHashReference(
   157→			plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(opts.FeatureBranch), headHash)
   158→		if err := repo.Storer.SetReference(ref); err != nil {
   159→			b.Fatalf("create branch: %v", err)
   160→		}
   161→		// Checkout via git CLI (go-git v5 checkout bug)
   162→		checkoutBranch(b, dir, opts.FeatureBranch)
   163→	}
   164→
   165→	br := &BenchRepo{
   166→		Dir:      dir,
   167→		Repo:     repo,
   168→		Store:    checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo),
   169→		HeadHash: headHash.String(),
   170→	}
   171→
   172→	// Determine worktree ID
   173→	wtID, err := paths.GetWorktreeID(dir)
   174→	if err == nil {
   175→		br.WorktreeID = wtID
   176→	}
   177→
   178→	return br
   179→}
   180→
   181→// WriteFile creates or overwrites a file relative to the repo root.
   182→func (br *BenchRepo) WriteFile(b *testing.B, relPath, content string) {
   183→	b.Helper()
   184→	writeFile(b, br.Dir, relPath, content)
   185→}
   186→
   187→// AddAndCommit stages the given files and creates a commit.
   188→// Returns the new HEAD hash.
   189→func (br *BenchRepo) AddAndCommit(b *testing.B, message string, files ...string) string {
   190→	b.Helper()
   191→	wt, err := br.Repo.Worktree()
   192→	if err != nil {
   193→		b.Fatalf("worktree: %v", err)
   194→	}
   195→	for _, f := range files {
   196→		if _, err := wt.Add(f); err != nil {
   197→			b.Fatalf("add %s: %v", f, err)
   198→		}
   199→	}
   200→	hash, err := wt.Commit(message, &git.CommitOptions{
   201→		Author: &object.Signature{
   202→			Name:  "Bench User",
   203→			Email: "bench@example.com",
   204→			When:  time.Now(),
   205→		},
   206→	})
   207→	if err != nil {
   208→		b.Fatalf("commit: %v", err)
   209→	}
   210→	br.HeadHash = hash.String()
   211→	return hash.String()
   212→}
   213→
   214→// SessionOpts configures how CreateSessionState creates a session state file.
   215→type SessionOpts struct {
   216→	// SessionID is the session identifier. Auto-generated if empty.
   217→	SessionID string
   218→
   219→	// Phase is the session phase. Defaults to session.PhaseActive.
   220→	Phase session.Phase
   221→
   222→	// StepCount is the number of prior checkpoints. Defaults to 0.
   223→	StepCount int
   224→
   225→	// FilesTouched is the list of files tracked by this session.
   226→	FilesTouched []string
   227→
   228→	// TranscriptPath is the path to the live transcript file.
   229→	TranscriptPath string
   230→
   231→	// AgentType is the agent type. Defaults to agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode.
   232→	AgentType agent.AgentType
   233→}
   234→
   235→// CreateSessionState writes a session state file to .git/entire-sessions/.
   236→// Returns the session ID used.
   237→func (br *BenchRepo) CreateSessionState(b *testing.B, opts SessionOpts) string {
   238→	b.Helper()
   239→
   240→	if opts.SessionID == "" {
   241→		cpID, err := id.Generate()
   242→		if err != nil {
   243→			b.Fatalf("generate session ID: %v", err)
   244→		}
   245→		opts.SessionID = fmt.Sprintf("bench-%s", cpID)
   246→	}
   247→	if opts.Phase == "" {
   248→		opts.Phase = session.PhaseActive
   249→	}
   250→
   251→	if opts.AgentType == "" {
   252→		opts.AgentType = agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode
   253→	}
   254→
   255→	now := time.Now()
   256→	state := &session.State{
   257→		SessionID:      opts.SessionID,
   258→		BaseCommit:     br.HeadHash,
   259→		WorktreePath:   br.Dir,
   260→		WorktreeID:     br.WorktreeID,
   261→		StartedAt:      now,
   262→		Phase:          opts.Phase,
   263→		StepCount:      opts.StepCount,
   264→		FilesTouched:   opts.FilesTouched,
   265→		TranscriptPath: opts.TranscriptPath,
   266→		AgentType:      opts.AgentType,
   267→	}
   268→
   269→	// Write to .git/entire-sessions/<session-id>.json
   270→	gitDir := filepath.Join(br.Dir, ".git")
   271→	sessDir := filepath.Join(gitDir, session.SessionStateDirName)
   272→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessDir, 0o750); err != nil {
   273→		b.Fatalf("mkdir sessions: %v", err)
   274→	}
   275→
   276→	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(state, "", "  ")
   277→	if err != nil {
   278→		b.Fatalf("marshal state: %v", err)
   279→	}
   280→
   281→	statePath := filepath.Join(sessDir, opts.SessionID+".json")
   282→	if err := os.WriteFile(statePath, data, 0o600); err != nil {
   283→		b.Fatalf("write state: %v", err)
   284→	}
   285→
   286→	return opts.SessionID
   287→}
   288→
   289→// TranscriptOpts configures how GenerateTranscript creates JSONL data.
   290→type TranscriptOpts struct {
   291→	// MessageCount is the number of JSONL messages to generate.
   292→	MessageCount int
   293→
   294→	// AvgMessageBytes is the approximate size of each message's content field.
   295→	// Defaults to 500.
   296→	AvgMessageBytes int
   297→
   298→	// IncludeToolUse adds realistic tool_use messages (file edits, bash commands).
   299→	IncludeToolUse bool
   300→
   301→	// FilesTouched is the list of files to reference in tool_use messages.
   302→	// Only used when IncludeToolUse is true.
   303→	FilesTouched []string
   304→}
   305→
   306→// GenerateTranscript creates realistic Claude Code JSONL transcript data.
   307→// Returns the raw bytes suitable for writing to full.jsonl.
   308→func GenerateTranscript(opts TranscriptOpts) []byte {
   309→	if opts.AvgMessageBytes == 0 {
   310→		opts.AvgMessageBytes = 500
   311→	}
   312→
   313→	var buf strings.Builder
   314→	for i := range opts.MessageCount {
   315→		msg := generateTranscriptMessage(i, opts)
   316→		data, err := json.Marshal(msg)
   317→		if err != nil {
   318→			// Should never happen with map[string]any, but satisfy errcheck
   319→			continue
   320→		}
   321→		buf.Write(data)
   322→		buf.WriteByte('\n')
   323→	}
   324→	return []byte(buf.String())
   325→}
   326→
   327→// WriteTranscriptFile writes transcript data to a file and returns the path.
   328→func (br *BenchRepo) WriteTranscriptFile(b *testing.B, sessionID string, data []byte) string {
   329→	b.Helper()
   330→	// Write to .entire/metadata/<session-id>/full.jsonl (matching real layout)
   331→	relDir := filepath.Join(".entire", "metadata", sessionID)
   332→	relPath := filepath.Join(relDir, "full.jsonl")
   333→	absDir := filepath.Join(br.Dir, relDir)
   334→	if err := os.MkdirAll(absDir, 0o750); err != nil {
   335→		b.Fatalf("mkdir transcript dir: %v", err)
   336→	}
   337→	absPath := filepath.Join(br.Dir, relPath)
   338→	if err := os.WriteFile(absPath, data, 0o600); err != nil {
   339→		b.Fatalf("write transcript: %v", err)
   340→	}
   341→	return absPath
   342→}
   343→
   344→// SeedShadowBranch creates N checkpoint commits on the shadow branch
   345→// for the current HEAD. This simulates a session that already has
   346→// prior checkpoints saved.
   347→func (br *BenchRepo) SeedShadowBranch(b *testing.B, sessionID string, checkpointCount int, filesPerCheckpoint int) {
   348→	b.Helper()
   349→
   350→	for i := range checkpointCount {
   351→		var modified []string
   352→		for j := range filesPerCheckpoint {
   353→			name := fmt.Sprintf("src/file_%03d.go", j)
   354→			content := GenerateGoFile(i*1000+j, 100)
   355→			writeFile(b, br.Dir, name, content)
   356→			modified = append(modified, name)
   357→		}
   358→
   359→		metadataDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   360→		metadataDirAbs := filepath.Join(br.Dir, metadataDir)
   361→		if err := os.MkdirAll(metadataDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   362→			b.Fatalf("mkdir metadata: %v", err)
   363→		}
   364→
   365→		// Write a minimal transcript to the metadata dir
   366→		transcriptPath := filepath.Join(metadataDirAbs, "full.jsonl")
   367→		transcript := GenerateTranscript(TranscriptOpts{MessageCount: 5, AvgMessageBytes: 200})
   368→		if err := os.WriteFile(transcriptPath, transcript, 0o600); err != nil {
   369→			b.Fatalf("write transcript: %v", err)
   370→		}
   371→
   372→		_, err := br.Store.WriteTemporary(context.Background(), checkpoint.WriteTemporaryOptions{
   373→			SessionID:         sessionID,
   374→			BaseCommit:        br.HeadHash,
   375→			WorktreeID:        br.WorktreeID,
   376→			ModifiedFiles:     modified,
   377→			MetadataDir:       metadataDir,
   378→			MetadataDirAbs:    metadataDirAbs,
   379→			CommitMessage:     fmt.Sprintf("Checkpoint %d", i+1),
   380→			AuthorName:        "Bench User",
   381→			AuthorEmail:       "bench@example.com",
   382→			IsFirstCheckpoint: i == 0,
   383→		})
   384→		if err != nil {
   385→			b.Fatalf("write temporary checkpoint %d: %v", i+1, err)
   386→		}
   387→	}
   388→}
   389→
   390→// SeedMetadataBranch creates N committed checkpoints on the entire/checkpoints/v1
   391→// branch. This simulates a repository with prior checkpoint history.
   392→func (br *BenchRepo) SeedMetadataBranch(b *testing.B, checkpointCount int) {
   393→	b.Helper()
   394→
   395→	for i := range checkpointCount {
   396→		cpID, err := id.Generate()
   397→		if err != nil {
   398→			b.Fatalf("generate checkpoint ID: %v", err)
   399→		}
   400→		sessionID := fmt.Sprintf("seed-session-%04d", i)
   401→		transcript := GenerateTranscript(TranscriptOpts{
   402→			MessageCount:    20,
   403→			AvgMessageBytes: 300,
   404→		})
   405→
   406→		files := make([]string, 0, 5)
   407→		for j := range 5 {
   408→			files = append(files, fmt.Sprintf("src/file_%03d.go", (i*5+j)%100))
   409→		}
   410→
   411→		err = br.Store.WriteCommitted(context.Background(), checkpoint.WriteCommittedOptions{
   412→			CheckpointID:     cpID,
   413→			SessionID:        sessionID,
   414→			Strategy:         "manual-commit",
   415→			Transcript:       transcript,
   416→			Prompts:          []string{fmt.Sprintf("Implement feature %d", i)},
   417→			FilesTouched:     files,
   418→			CheckpointsCount: 3,
   419→			AuthorName:       "Bench User",
   420→			AuthorEmail:      "bench@example.com",
   421→			Agent:            agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode,
   422→		})
   423→		if err != nil {
   424→			b.Fatalf("write committed checkpoint %d: %v", i+1, err)
   425→		}
   426→	}
   427→}
   428→
   429→// GenerateGoFile creates a synthetic Go source file with the given number of lines.
   430→// The seed value ensures unique content for each file.
   431→func GenerateGoFile(seed, lines int) string {
   432→	var buf strings.Builder
   433→	fmt.Fprintf(&buf, "package pkg%d\n\n", seed%100)
   434→
   435→	lineNum := 2
   436→	funcNum := 0
   437→	for lineNum < lines {
   438→		funcName := fmt.Sprintf("func%d_%d", seed, funcNum)
   439→		fmt.Fprintf(&buf, "func %s(ctx context.Context, input string) (string, error) {\n", funcName)
   440→		lineNum++
   441→
   442→		bodyLines := min(8, lines-lineNum-1)
   443→		for j := range bodyLines {
   444→			fmt.Fprintf(&buf, "\tv%d := fmt.Sprintf(\"processing %%s step %d seed %d\", input)\n", j, j, seed)
   445→			lineNum++
   446→		}
   447→		buf.WriteString("\treturn \"\", nil\n}\n\n")
   448→		lineNum += 2
   449→		funcNum++
   450→	}
   451→	return buf.String()
   452→}
   453→
   454→// GenerateFileContent creates generic file content of approximately the given byte size.
   455→func GenerateFileContent(seed, sizeBytes int) string {
   456→	var buf strings.Builder
   457→	line := fmt.Sprintf("// Line content seed=%d ", seed)
   458→	padding := strings.Repeat("x", max(1, 80-len(line)))
   459→	fullLine := line + padding + "\n"
   460→
   461→	for buf.Len() < sizeBytes {
   462→		buf.WriteString(fullLine)
   463→	}
   464→	return buf.String()
   465→}

   471→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(abs), 0o755); err != nil {
   472→		b.Fatalf("mkdir %s: %v", filepath.Dir(relPath), err)
   473→	}
   474→	if err := os.WriteFile(abs, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil {
   475→		b.Fatalf("write %s: %v", relPath, err)

   477→}

   500→func checkoutBranch(b *testing.B, dir, branch string) {
   501→	b.Helper()
   502→	c := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), "git", "checkout", branch)
   503→	c.Dir = dir

   506→	}

   508→
   509→// generateTranscriptMessage creates a single JSONL message for a Claude Code transcript.
   510→func generateTranscriptMessage(index int, opts TranscriptOpts) map[string]any {
   511→	msg := map[string]any{
   512→		"uuid":        fmt.Sprintf("msg_%06d", index),
   513→		"timestamp":   time.Now().Add(time.Duration(index) * time.Second).Format(time.RFC3339),
   514→		"parent_uuid": fmt.Sprintf("msg_%06d", max(0, index-1)),
   515→	}
   516→
   517→	switch {
   518→	case opts.IncludeToolUse && index%3 == 2 && len(opts.FilesTouched) > 0:
   519→		// Tool use message (every 3rd message)
   520→		file := opts.FilesTouched[index%len(opts.FilesTouched)]
   521→		msg["type"] = "tool_use"
   522→		msg["tool_name"] = "write_to_file"
   523→		msg["tool_input"] = map[string]any{
   524→			"path":    file,
   525→			"content": GenerateFileContent(index, opts.AvgMessageBytes/2),
   526→		}
   527→	case index%2 == 0:
   528→		// Assistant message
   529→		msg["type"] = "assistant"
   530→		msg["content"] = generatePadding("I'll help you implement this feature. ", opts.AvgMessageBytes)
   531→	default:
   532→		// Human message
   533→		msg["type"] = "human"
   534→		msg["content"] = generatePadding("Please update the implementation. ", opts.AvgMessageBytes/3)
   535→	}
   536→
   537→	return msg
   538→}
   539→
   540→func generatePadding(prefix string, targetBytes int) string {
   541→	if len(prefix) >= targetBytes {
   542→		return prefix[:targetBytes]
   543→	}
   544→	padding := strings.Repeat("Lorem ipsum dolor sit amet. ", (targetBytes-len(prefix))/28+1)
   545→	result := prefix + padding
   546→	if len(result) > targetBytes {
   547→		return result[:targetBytes]
   548→	}
   549→	return result
   550→}
   551→
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/evisdrenova/code/entire/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/hooks.go",
  "old_string": "// GetHooksDir returns the active hooks directory path.\n// This respects core.hooksPath and correctly resolves to the common hooks\n// directory when called from a linked worktree.\nfunc GetHooksDir() (string, error) {\n\treturn getHooksDirInPath(\".\")\n}",
  "new_string": "var (\n\thooksDirMu       sync.RWMutex\n\thooksDirCache    string\n\thooksDirCacheDir string\n)\n\n// GetHooksDir returns the active hooks directory path.\n// This respects core.hooksPath and correctly resolves to the common hooks\n// directory when called from a linked worktree.\n// Results are cached per CWD to avoid repeated subprocess spawns.\nfunc GetHooksDir() (string, error) {\n\tcwd, err := os.Getwd()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tcwd = \"\"\n\t}\n\n\thooksDirMu.RLock()\n\tif hooksDirCache != \"\" && hooksDirCacheDir == cwd {\n\t\tcached := hooksDirCache\n\t\thooksDirMu.RUnlock()\n\t\treturn cached, nil\n\t}\n\thooksDirMu.RUnlock()\n\n\tresult, err := getHooksDirInPath(\".\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", err\n\t}\n\n\thooksDirMu.Lock()\n\thooksDirCache = result\n\thooksDirCacheDir = cwd\n\thooksDirMu.Unlock()\n\n\treturn result, nil\n}\n\n// ClearHooksDirCache resets the cached hooks directory.\n// Used by tests that change the working directory.\nfunc ClearHooksDirCache() {\n\thooksDirMu.Lock()\n\thooksDirCache = \"\"\n\thooksDirCacheDir = \"\"\n\thooksDirMu.Unlock()\n}"
}
```

```json
{
```

```json
  "old_string": "func OpenRepository() (*git.Repository, error) {\n\t// First, find the repository root using git rev-parse --show-toplevel\n\t// This works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository\n\trepoRoot, err := GetWorktreePath()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t// Fallback to current directory if git command fails\n\t\t// (e.g., if git is not installed or we're not in a repo)\n\t\trepoRoot = \".\"\n\t}",
  "new_string": "func OpenRepository() (*git.Repository, error) {\n\t// Use paths.RepoRoot() which caches the result per CWD,\n\t// avoiding repeated `git rev-parse --show-toplevel` subprocess spawns.\n\trepoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t// Fallback to current directory if git command fails\n\t\t// (e.g., if git is not installed or we're not in a repo)\n\t\trepoRoot = \".\"\n\t}"
}
```

> AGENT

The linter wants `b.Chdir()` instead of `os.Chdir()`. However, per CLAUDE.md, `t.Chdir()`/`b.Chdir()` panics when used after `t.Parallel()` — and more importantly, `b.Chdir()` auto-restores on cleanup which may conflict with our manual restore pattern. Let me check if `b.Chdir()` works correctly in benchmark loops.

Since `b.Chdir()` changes the directory and restores it at cleanup, I can't use it inside the loop (it would restore to the wrong dir after each sub-benchmark). But I can restructure to use it properly — call `b.Chdir()` once per sub-benchmark, which handles restore automatically.

I need to suppress the lint for the loop-based chdir calls and use `b.Chdir()` where it makes sense (the ReEnable case where we chdir once).

| Scenario | Time | Memory | Allocs |
|---|---|---|---|
| **NewRepo** (fresh enable) | ~41ms | 962KB | 1,131 |
| **ReEnable** (already enabled) | ~37ms | 246KB | 855 |

The benchmark file is at `cmd/entire/cli/bench_enable_test.go`. It exercises `setupAgentHooksNonInteractive()` — the exact code path for `entire enable --agent claude-code`. The NewRepo scenario creates a fresh git repo each iteration, while ReEnable reuses an already-enabled repo. Ready for you to review before we move on to optimizations.

> AGENT

You can use the existing `bench:compare` task with the `BENCH_PATTERN` env var:

```bash
BENCH_PATTERN=BenchmarkEnableCommand mise run bench:compare
```

This will:
1. Run the enable benchmark on your current branch
2. Stash changes, checkout main, run it there
3. Switch back and show a `benchstat` comparison

> AGENT

Two issues here:

1. **Parsing errors**: The benchmark prints `✓ Created orphan branch` to stderr, and the `bench:compare` script captures stderr into the output file (`2>&1`), which confuses benchstat.
2. **n=1 samples**: Same root cause — corrupted lines mean benchstat can only parse 1 sample per benchmark.

The fix is in `mise.toml` — discard stderr instead of mixing it into the benchmark output.

> AGENT

Yes, the `2>/dev/null` fix addresses both issues:

1. **Invalid syntax** — caused by `✓ Created orphan branch...` stderr lines getting mixed into the benchmark output file. benchstat tried to parse them as benchmark data. Now stderr is discarded.
2. **n=1 samples** — same root cause. With clean output, benchstat will see all 6 samples per run and give proper confidence intervals.

The column header formatting (long temp dir paths) is just how benchstat displays file names — that's cosmetic and not something we control without additional flags.

Go ahead and run it:

```bash
BENCH_PATTERN=BenchmarkEnableCommand mise run bench:compare
```

Three fixes:

1. **Parsing errors** — pipe `go test` output through `grep` to keep only benchmark lines (`Benchmark...`, `goos:`, `goarch:`, etc.), stripping `ok`, `PASS`, and any other noise
2. **Column headers** — added `-col .name` to benchstat so it shows `old.txt` / `new.txt` instead of full temp paths
3. **Package targeting** — added `BENCH_PKG` env var (defaults to `./...`) so you can scope to a single package

Now run:

```bash
BENCH_PATTERN=BenchmarkEnableCommand BENCH_PKG=./cmd/entire/cli/ mise run bench:compare
```

> AGENT

There it is — `EnsureMetadataBranch` writes to `os.Stderr` directly, but `go test` captures it as **stdout** because test binary stderr and stdout get merged. So `2>/dev/null` in the shell doesn't help — these lines come out on stdout.

The simplest fix: just drop the `bench:compare` complexity and write a self-contained script that does exactly what you want.

> TOOL

> AGENT

That works perfectly. So the grep is correct — the issue is that on `main` the benchmark file doesn't exist so `go test` fails and produces garbage. When it checks out main, there's no `bench_enable_test.go`.

That's the fundamental issue — the benchmark file only exists on the feature branch, not main. So the `bench:compare` approach of switching branches won't work here. Let me rewrite this to run the current branch benchmark N times and just report clean results. For before/after comparison you run it once before optimizations, note the numbers, then again after.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_016VLWwgDF2YuM1BWDWv7dU6
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/evisdrenova/code/entire/devenv/cli/mise.toml",
  "old_string": "[tasks.\"bench:compare\"]\ndescription = \"Compare benchmarks between current branch and main\"\nrun = \"\"\"\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -euo pipefail\n\n# Install benchstat if not available\nif ! command -v benchstat &>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"Installing benchstat...\"\n  if ! go install golang.org/x/perf/cmd/benchstat@latest; then\n    echo \"Failed to install benchstat. Please install it manually: go install golang.org/x/perf/cmd/benchstat@latest\"\n    exit 1\n  fi\nfi\n\nBENCH_PATTERN=\"${BENCH_PATTERN:-.}\"\nBENCH_PKG=\"${BENCH_PKG:-./...}\"\nBENCH_COUNT=\"${BENCH_COUNT:-6}\"\nBENCH_TIMEOUT=\"${BENCH_TIMEOUT:-10m}\"\nBASE_REF=\"${BASE_REF:-main}\"\n\ncurrent_branch=$(git rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD)\nif [ \"$current_branch\" = \"$BASE_REF\" ]; then\n  echo \"Already on $BASE_REF — nothing to compare. Run from a feature branch.\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n\ntmpdir=$(mktemp -d)\nnew_out=\"$tmpdir/new.txt\"\nold_out=\"$tmpdir/old.txt\"\n\nhas_changes=false\ntrap 'git checkout \"$current_branch\" --quiet 2>/dev/null; [ \"$has_changes\" = true ] && git stash pop --quiet 2>/dev/null; rm -rf \"$tmpdir\"' EXIT\n\necho \"=== Benchmarking current branch ($current_branch) ===\"\necho \"  pattern=$BENCH_PATTERN  pkg=$BENCH_PKG  count=$BENCH_COUNT\"\ngo test -bench=\"$BENCH_PATTERN\" -benchmem -run='^$' -count=\"$BENCH_COUNT\" -timeout=\"$BENCH_TIMEOUT\" \"$BENCH_PKG\" 2>/dev/null \\\n  | grep -E '^(Benchmark|goos:|goarch:|pkg:|cpu:)' > \"$new_out\" || true\n\n# Check for uncommitted changes\nhas_changes=false\nif ! git diff --quiet || ! git diff --cached --quiet; then\n  has_changes=true\n  echo \"Stashing uncommitted changes...\"\n  git stash push -m \"bench:compare auto-stash\"\nfi\n\necho \"\"\necho \"=== Benchmarking base ($BASE_REF) ===\"\ngit checkout \"$BASE_REF\" --quiet\ngo test -bench=\"$BENCH_PATTERN\" -benchmem -run='^$' -count=\"$BENCH_COUNT\" -timeout=\"$BENCH_TIMEOUT\" \"$BENCH_PKG\" 2>/dev/null \\\n  | grep -E '^(Benchmark|goos:|goarch:|pkg:|cpu:)' > \"$old_out\" || true\n\necho \"\"\necho \"=== Switching back to $current_branch ===\"\ngit checkout \"$current_branch\" --quiet\nif [ \"$has_changes\" = true ]; then\n  git stash pop --quiet\nfi\n\necho \"\"\necho \"=== Results (base=$BASE_REF vs current=$current_branch) ===\"\necho \"\"\nbenchstat -col .name \"$old_out\" \"$new_out\"\n\"\"\"",
  "new_string": "[tasks.\"bench:compare\"]\ndescription = \"Compare benchmarks between current branch and base ref\"\nrun = \"\"\"\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -euo pipefail\n\nBENCH_PATTERN=\"${BENCH_PATTERN:-.}\"\nBENCH_PKG=\"${BENCH_PKG:-./...}\"\nBENCH_COUNT=\"${BENCH_COUNT:-6}\"\nBENCH_TIMEOUT=\"${BENCH_TIMEOUT:-10m}\"\nBASE_REF=\"${BASE_REF:-main}\"\n\ncurrent_branch=$(git rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD)\nif [ \"$current_branch\" = \"$BASE_REF\" ]; then\n  echo \"Already on $BASE_REF — nothing to compare. Run from a feature branch.\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n\ntmpdir=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap 'rm -rf \"$tmpdir\"' EXIT\n\nrun_bench() {\n  local label=\"$1\" out=\"$2\"\n  echo \"=== Benchmarking: $label ===\"\n  go test -bench=\"$BENCH_PATTERN\" -benchmem -run='^$' -count=\"$BENCH_COUNT\" -timeout=\"$BENCH_TIMEOUT\" \"$BENCH_PKG\" 2>/dev/null \\\n    | grep -E '^(Benchmark|goos:|goarch:|pkg:|cpu:)' > \"$out\"\n  local count\n  count=$(grep -c '^Benchmark' \"$out\" || true)\n  if [ \"$count\" -eq 0 ]; then\n    echo \"  ERROR: no benchmark results captured. Does the benchmark exist on this branch?\"\n    return 1\n  fi\n  echo \"  captured $count benchmark lines\"\n}\n\n# Run on current branch first\nnew_out=\"$tmpdir/new.txt\"\nif ! run_bench \"$current_branch\" \"$new_out\"; then\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n# Stash if needed, switch to base, run, switch back\nhas_changes=false\nif ! git diff --quiet || ! git diff --cached --quiet; then\n  has_changes=true\n  git stash push -q -m \"bench:compare auto-stash\"\nfi\n\nold_out=\"$tmpdir/old.txt\"\necho \"\"\ngit checkout \"$BASE_REF\" --quiet 2>/dev/null\nif ! run_bench \"$BASE_REF\" \"$old_out\"; then\n  echo \"\"\n  echo \"Benchmark does not exist on $BASE_REF. Showing current branch results only:\"\n  echo \"\"\n  git checkout \"$current_branch\" --quiet 2>/dev/null\n  [ \"$has_changes\" = true ] && git stash pop --quiet\n\n  # Pretty-print single-branch results\n  printf \"%-40s %12s %12s %12s\\n\" \"Benchmark\" \"ms/op\" \"B/op\" \"allocs/op\"\n  printf \"%-40s %12s %12s %12s\\n\" \"----------------------------------------\" \"--------\" \"--------\" \"---------\"\n  grep '^Benchmark' \"$new_out\" | while read -r name iters nsop _ bop _ aop _; do\n    ms=$(echo \"scale=2; $nsop / 1000000\" | bc)\n    printf \"%-40s %10s ms %12s %12s\\n\" \"$name\" \"$ms\" \"$bop\" \"$aop\"\n  done\n  exit 0\nfi\n\ngit checkout \"$current_branch\" --quiet 2>/dev/null\n[ \"$has_changes\" = true ] && git stash pop --quiet\n\n# Both branches have results — show comparison table\necho \"\"\necho \"=== Results: $BASE_REF vs $current_branch ===\"\necho \"\"\n\n# Build comparison table\nprintf \"%-40s │ %14s │ %14s │ %8s\\n\" \"Benchmark\" \"$BASE_REF\" \"$current_branch\" \"delta\"\nprintf \"%-40s─┼─%14s─┼─%14s─┼─%8s\\n\" \"$(printf '─%.0s' {1..40})\" \"$(printf '─%.0s' {1..14})\" \"$(printf '─%.0s' {1..14})\" \"$(printf '─%.0s' {1..8})\"\n\ngrep '^Benchmark' \"$new_out\" | while read -r name iters nsop _ bop _ aop _; do\n  new_ms=$(echo \"scale=2; $nsop / 1000000\" | bc)\n  # Find matching line in old\n  old_line=$(grep \"^$name\" \"$old_out\" | head -1)\n  if [ -n \"$old_line\" ]; then\n    old_nsop=$(echo \"$old_line\" | awk '{print $3}')\n    old_ms=$(echo \"scale=2; $old_nsop / 1000000\" | bc)\n    if [ \"$(echo \"$old_ms > 0\" | bc)\" -eq 1 ]; then\n      delta=$(echo \"scale=1; ($new_ms - $old_ms) / $old_ms * 100\" | bc)\n      printf \"%-40s │ %11s ms │ %11s ms │ %+7s%%\\n\" \"$name\" \"$old_ms\" \"$new_ms\" \"$delta\"\n    else\n      printf \"%-40s │ %11s ms │ %11s ms │ %8s\\n\" \"$name\" \"$old_ms\" \"$new_ms\" \"n/a\"\n    fi\n  else\n    printf \"%-40s │ %14s │ %11s ms │ %8s\\n\" \"$name\" \"(new)\" \"$new_ms\" \"n/a\"\n  fi\ndone\n\"\"\""
}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/evisdrenova/code/entire/devenv/cli/mise.toml",
  "old_string": "# Both branches have results — show comparison table\necho \"\"\necho \"=== Results: $BASE_REF vs $current_branch ===\"\necho \"\"\n\n# Build comparison table\nprintf \"%-40s │ %14s │ %14s │ %8s\\n\" \"Benchmark\" \"$BASE_REF\" \"$current_branch\" \"delta\"\nprintf \"%-40s─┼─%14s─┼─%14s─┼─%8s\\n\" \"$(printf '─%.0s' {1..40})\" \"$(printf '─%.0s' {1..14})\" \"$(printf '─%.0s' {1..14})\" \"$(printf '─%.0s' {1..8})\"\n\ngrep '^Benchmark' \"$new_out\" | while read -r name iters nsop _ bop _ aop _; do\n  new_ms=$(echo \"scale=2; $nsop / 1000000\" | bc)\n  # Find matching line in old\n  old_line=$(grep \"^$name\" \"$old_out\" | head -1)\n  if [ -n \"$old_line\" ]; then\n    old_nsop=$(echo \"$old_line\" | awk '{print $3}')\n    old_ms=$(echo \"scale=2; $old_nsop / 1000000\" | bc)\n    if [ \"$(echo \"$old_ms > 0\" | bc)\" -eq 1 ]; then\n      delta=$(echo \"scale=1; ($new_ms - $old_ms) / $old_ms * 100\" | bc)\n      printf \"%-40s │ %11s ms │ %11s ms │ %+7s%%\\n\" \"$name\" \"$old_ms\" \"$new_ms\" \"$delta\"\n    else\n      printf \"%-40s │ %11s ms │ %11s ms │ %8s\\n\" \"$name\" \"$old_ms\" \"$new_ms\" \"n/a\"\n    fi\n  else\n    printf \"%-40s │ %14s │ %11s ms │ %8s\\n\" \"$name\" \"(new)\" \"$new_ms\" \"n/a\"\n  fi\ndone",
  "new_string": "# Both branches have results — show comparison table\necho \"\"\npython3 - \"$old_out\" \"$new_out\" \"$BASE_REF\" \"$current_branch\" <<'PYEOF'\nimport sys, re\n\nold_file, new_file, base_name, curr_name = sys.argv[1:5]\n\ndef parse_benchmarks(path):\n    results = {}\n    for line in open(path):\n        if not line.startswith(\"Benchmark\"):\n            continue\n        parts = line.split()\n        # name iters ns/op \"ns/op\" B/op \"B/op\" allocs/op \"allocs/op\"\n        if len(parts) >= 8:\n            name = parts[0]\n            ns = float(parts[2])\n            bop = int(parts[4])\n            allocs = int(parts[6])\n            results[name] = (ns / 1e6, bop, allocs)\n    return results\n\nold = parse_benchmarks(old_file)\nnew = parse_benchmarks(new_file)\nall_names = list(dict.fromkeys(list(new.keys()) + list(old.keys())))\n\n# Header\nprint(f\"{'Benchmark':<45} {'ms/op':>18} {'B/op':>18} {'allocs/op':>18}\")\nprint(f\"{'':─<45}─{'':─<18}─{'':─<18}─{'':─<18}\")\n\nfor name in all_names:\n    short = name.replace(\"BenchmarkEnableCommand/\", \"\").replace(\"Benchmark\", \"\")\n    if name in old and name in new:\n        o_ms, o_b, o_a = old[name]\n        n_ms, n_b, n_a = new[name]\n        d_ms = ((n_ms - o_ms) / o_ms * 100) if o_ms > 0 else 0\n        d_b = ((n_b - o_b) / o_b * 100) if o_b > 0 else 0\n        d_a = ((n_a - o_a) / o_a * 100) if o_a > 0 else 0\n        def fmt_delta(d):\n            return f\"({d:+.1f}%)\"\n        print(f\"  {short:<43} {o_ms:>6.1f} → {n_ms:>6.1f} {fmt_delta(d_ms):>7}  {o_b:>7} → {n_b:>7} {fmt_delta(d_b):>7}  {o_a:>5} → {n_a:>5} {fmt_delta(d_a):>7}\")\n    elif name in new:\n        n_ms, n_b, n_a = new[name]\n        print(f\"  {short:<43} {'':>6}   {n_ms:>6.1f} {'(new)':>7}  {'':>7}   {n_b:>7} {'(new)':>7}  {'':>5}   {n_a:>5} {'(new)':>7}\")\n\nprint()\nprint(f\"  base: {base_name}    current: {curr_name}\")\nPYEOF"
}
```

The grep is clean — the `✓` line doesn't pass through. The problem is that when switching to `main`, the stashed changes get applied but the `go test` output still contains those lines.   "file_path": "/Users/evisdrenova/code/entire/devenv/cli/mise.toml",
```json
  "old_string": "def parse_benchmarks(path):\n    results = {}\n    for line in open(path):\n        if not line.startswith(\"Benchmark\"):\n            continue\n        parts = line.split()\n        # name iters ns/op \"ns/op\" B/op \"B/op\" allocs/op \"allocs/op\"\n        if len(parts) >= 8:\n            name = parts[0]\n            ns = float(parts[2])\n            bop = int(parts[4])\n            allocs = int(parts[6])\n            results[name] = (ns / 1e6, bop, allocs)\n    return results",
  "new_string": "def parse_benchmarks(path):\n    results = {}\n    for line in open(path):\n        if not line.startswith(\"Benchmark\"):\n            continue\n        parts = line.split()\n        if len(parts) < 8:\n            continue\n        try:\n            name = parts[0]\n            ns = float(parts[2])\n            bop = int(parts[4])\n            allocs = int(parts[6])\n            results[name] = (ns / 1e6, bop, allocs)\n        except (ValueError, IndexError):\n            continue\n    return results"
}
```
